# Phase 1 — Base: Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking. Executors are Claude Haiku agents that **decide nothing**: read `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-00-architecture.md` §11 first.

**Goal:** Stand up the Gomez ERP application (Laravel 13 + React/Inertia, PostgreSQL, Laravel Boost) with Google Workspace login, users/roles/permissions with audited impersonation, an append-only activity log, a Spanish app shell, all master data from spec §3.2, exchange rates with a swappable source, generic xlsx/csv export and Excel importers with per-row preview.

**Architecture:** Standard Laravel 13 app on the official React starter kit (Inertia 3, React 19, TypeScript, Tailwind 4, shadcn new-york, Wayfinder). Business rules live in Actions/Services; controllers only validate (FormRequests), authorize (`#[Middleware('permission:…')]` + policies) and render Inertia pages. Master data is plain Eloquent with activity logging. Everything money-related goes through `App\Support\Money\Decimal` (brick/math, HALF_UP).

**Tech Stack:** PHP 8.4, Laravel 13.39, laravel/react-starter-kit `dev-main`, PostgreSQL 17 (Docker), laravel/boost 2.10, laravel/socialite 5.31, spatie/laravel-permission 8.3, spatie/laravel-activitylog 5.1, maatwebsite/excel 4.0, brick/money 0.15, Pest 5.3, shadcn CLI 4.23.0.

**Spec:** `/Users/shalemperez/www/gomezintsystem/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-29-gomez-erp-design.md` — this plan implements §3.1, §3.2, §3.11 (exports only), §3.12 (master-data importers only), §3.14, §9 item 1.

**Architecture doc (inherited):** `/Users/shalemperez/www/gomezintsystem/docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-00-architecture.md` (cited below as *ARCH §n*).

**Branch:** `phase-1-base` (already created from `main` by the coordinator; Task 1 is complete at commit `d97c437`).

## Global Constraints

Every task implicitly includes this section. Executors copy names, strings and commands verbatim.

### GC-1 Environment, gates and commits
- Repo root: `/Users/shalemperez/www/gomezintsystem`. All commands run there unless stated. Use absolute paths in tool calls.
- PHP = Herd's `php` (8.4.27). Composer 2.10. Node 22 / npm 10. Docker Compose v5.
- Dev URL: `http://gomezintsystem.test` (Herd parks `~/www`). DB: Docker Compose service `db`, host `127.0.0.1`, port `5435`, user `gomez`, password `gomez`, databases `gomez` (dev) and `gomez_test` (tests). `psql` is used as `docker compose exec -T db psql -U gomez -d gomez -c "<SQL>"`.
- **Test output format.** In this headless environment `php artisan test` and `composer test` print one JSON summary line per tool, e.g. `{"tool":"pint","result":"passed"}`, `{"tool":"phpstan","result":"passed","errors":0}`, `{"tool":"phpunit","result":"passed","tests":23,"passed":21,"assertions":53,"duration_ms":1511,"skipped":2}`. Wherever this plan writes `Expected: … passed (N tests)` the pass condition is: every tool line has `"result":"passed"`, the phpunit line has no `"failed"` key or `"failed":0`, and `"tests":N` where N is the planner's count — **a different N is NOT a blocker** as long as nothing failed and the task's own tests ran; any failure is always a blocker. Where a step expects failures (TDD RED), the pass condition is `"result":"failed"` on the phpunit line.
- **Task gate (every task, last step before the commit):** `vendor/bin/pint --dirty && composer test 2>&1 | tail -6` → Expected: pint `"result":"passed"`, phpstan `"result":"passed","errors":0`, phpunit `"result":"passed"` with the task's N. When TSX/TS changed also run `npm run check:fix && npm run types:check` (no errors) and, when the task says so, `npm run build` (last line `✓ built in …`).
- **Commit rule.** A step written as `Commit — <subject>` means exactly:
  ```bash
  vendor/bin/pint --dirty && git add -A && git commit -q -m "<subject>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Haiku 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" && git log --oneline -1
  ```
  Expected: pint `"result":"passed"` and one line `<hash> <subject>`. Never commit with a failing gate.

### GC-2 Language, locale, formats
- Code/identifiers/routes/commits: English. UI/messages/emails: Spanish (es-DO), accents required, sentence case, no trailing period on labels/buttons.
- `APP_LOCALE=es`, `APP_FALLBACK_LOCALE=es`, `APP_FAKER_LOCALE=es_ES`, `APP_TIMEZONE=America/Santo_Domingo`.
- Dates in UI `dd/MM/yyyy` (`formatDate`), money `formatMoney(value, 'DOP'|'USD')`, numbers `formatNumber(value, decimals)` from `@/lib/format` (Task 8).

### GC-3 Database (ARCH §3)
- Amount `decimal(14,2)`; qty/unit price `decimal(14,4)`; FX rate `decimal(12,6)`; percent `decimal(7,4)`; physical measures `decimal(10,4)`. Exceptions declared in this plan: `products.itbis_rate decimal(7,2)` and `products.alcohol_degree decimal(5,2)` (Task 17). Eloquent casts `decimal:N` (strings). No floats anywhere in PHP money code.
- Every table except pivot tables and `import_batch_rows`: `id()`, `timestamps()`. Master data: `is_active boolean default true`. FKs: `foreignId('x_id')->constrained('<table>')` + explicit `restrictOnDelete()` / `cascadeOnDelete()` / `nullOnDelete()` as the task states.
- Migration file names: `2026_10_29_<6-digit sequence>_<description>.php` with the sequence given in the task. Published vendor migrations are renamed to the sequence the task gives. Never modify a migration from an earlier task; add a new one.
- Controllers catch `Illuminate\Database\QueryException` with `$e->getCode() === '23503'` on `destroy()` and redirect back with `->with('error', 'No se puede eliminar: el registro está en uso')`.
- Deleting child rows that must be audited is always done through models (`$relation->get()->each->delete()`), never with a bulk `->delete()` query.

### GC-4 Authorization
- Spatie permission names from ARCH §5.3. Each controller method carries `#[Middleware('permission:<name>')]` (`use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;`). Route groups carry `['auth']` (the `active` middleware is global in the `web` group).
- `super-admin` bypasses everything via `Gate::before` (Task 5).
- Row-level rules via Policies registered automatically by name (`App\Policies\<Model>Policy`).

### GC-5 Flash, toasts, errors
- Success: `return redirect()->route('<route>')->with('success', '<Spanish>');`. Error: `->with('error', '<Spanish>')`. The layout shows them as toasts (Task 8 `useFlashMessages`). Validation errors show under fields via `InputError`.
- Standard success strings per resource: `"<Singular> creado"` / `"<Singular> actualizado"` / `"<Singular> eliminado"` with gender agreement given in each task (e.g. "Marca creada").

### GC-6 Shared React building blocks (Task 8 creates them; later tasks consume them)
- `@/components/erp/page-header` — `<PageHeader title="…" description?="…" actions?={<ReactNode>} />`.
- `@/components/erp/data-table` — props type `DataTableProps<Row>`:
  ```ts
  export type Column<Row> = { key: string; label: string; sortable?: boolean; className?: string; render?: (row: Row) => ReactNode };
  export type Paginated<Row> = { data: Row[]; current_page: number; last_page: number; per_page: number; total: number; from: number | null; to: number | null; links: { url: string | null; label: string; active: boolean }[] };
  export type DataTableProps<Row extends { id: number }> = {
    columns: Column<Row>[]; rows: Paginated<Row>; routeUrl: string;      // routeUrl = index URL (e.g. index.url())
    filters: Record<string, string | undefined>;                            // current query values: search, sort, dir, per_page + custom
    searchPlaceholder?: string; emptyText?: string;
    rowActions?: (row: Row) => ReactNode; toolbar?: ReactNode;              // toolbar renders left of the search box (filter selects)
  };
  ```
  Behaviour: search input (debounced 300 ms) → `router.get(routeUrl, {...filters, search, page: undefined}, {preserveState: true, preserveScroll: true, replace: true})` (the page is reset by omitting it); sortable header click toggles `sort`/`dir`; pagination links; `per_page` select with values 15/25/50/100 (the backend accepts only those; anything else → 15); shows "Mostrando {from}–{to} de {total}" and `emptyText` (default "No hay registros").
- `@/components/erp/form-field` — `<FormField label="…" htmlFor="…" error={errors.x} required? hint?>{children}</FormField>`.
- `@/components/erp/export-menu` — `<ExportMenu xlsxUrl={…} csvUrl={…} />` (dropdown "Exportar" → "Excel (.xlsx)", "CSV").
- `@/components/erp/confirm-delete` — `<ConfirmDelete url={destroy.url(id)} title="Eliminar <singular>" description="Esta acción no se puede deshacer." />` (AlertDialog; buttons "Cancelar"/"Eliminar").
- `@/components/erp/active-badge` — `<ActiveBadge active={bool} />` → "Activo"/"Inactivo".
- `@/components/erp/record-history` — `<RecordHistory history={AuditEntry[]} />` renders the audit trail of one record (Fecha, Usuario [+ "suplantado por …"], Evento, Descripción, Cambios as a field/old/new table). Type `AuditEntry` in `@/types/audit` (Task 8).
- Backend `App\Support\AuditHistory::for(Model $model, int $limit = 100): array` returns `AuditEntry[]` (latest first) for the edit pages' "Historial" tab.
- `@/lib/can` — `useCan(): (permission: string) => boolean`.
- Index query backend helper `App\Support\TableQuery::apply(Builder $q, Request $r, array $searchable, array $sortable, string $defaultSort = 'id', string $defaultDir = 'desc'): \Illuminate\Pagination\LengthAwarePaginator` (Task 8): applies `search` (ILIKE on each searchable column, OR), `sort`/`dir` (whitelisted), `per_page` (15 default; only 15/25/50/100), `->withQueryString()`.

### GC-7 Resource pattern (used by every CRUD task; the task lists only what differs)

**Routes** (`routes/<area>.php`, required from `routes/web.php`):
```php
Route::middleware(['auth'])->prefix('<area>')->name('<area>.')->group(function () {
    Route::get('<plural>/export', [<Model>Controller::class, 'export'])->name('<plural>.export');
    Route::resource('<plural>', <Model>Controller::class)->except(['show']);
});
```
Route names: `<area>.<plural>.{index,create,store,edit,update,destroy,export}`.

**Controller** `app/Http/Controllers/<Area>/<Model>Controller.php` — template (replace `<…>`):
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\<Area>;

use App\Exports\TableExport;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\<Area>\Store<Model>Request;
use App\Http\Requests\<Area>\Update<Model>Request;
use App\Models\<Model>;
use App\Support\AuditHistory;
use App\Support\TableQuery;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\BinaryFileResponse;

class <Model>Controller extends Controller
{
    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.view')]
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $rows = TableQuery::apply(<Model>::query()<with()>, $request, [<searchable>], [<sortable>], '<defaultSort>', '<defaultDir>');

        return Inertia::render('<area>/<plural-kebab>/index', [
            'rows' => $rows,
            'filters' => $request->only(['search', 'sort', 'dir', 'per_page'<, custom filters>]),
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.create')]
    public function create(): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('<area>/<plural-kebab>/form', [<form options>]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.create')]
    public function store(Store<Model>Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        <Model>::create($request->validated());

        return redirect()->route('<area>.<plural-kebab>.index')->with('success', '<Singular> creado');
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.update')]
    public function edit(<Model> $<model>): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('<area>/<plural-kebab>/form', ['<model>' => $<model>, 'history' => AuditHistory::for($<model>), <form options>]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.update')]
    public function update(Update<Model>Request $request, <Model> $<model>): RedirectResponse
    {
        $<model>->update($request->validated());

        return redirect()->route('<area>.<plural-kebab>.index')->with('success', '<Singular> actualizado');
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.delete')]
    public function destroy(<Model> $<model>): RedirectResponse
    {
        try {
            $<model>->delete();
        } catch (QueryException $e) {
            if ($e->getCode() === '23503') {
                return back()->with('error', 'No se puede eliminar: el registro está en uso');
            }
            throw $e;
        }

        return redirect()->route('<area>.<plural-kebab>.index')->with('success', '<Singular> eliminado');
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:<module>.<plural-kebab>.export')]
    public function export(Request $request): BinaryFileResponse
    {
        $query = TableQuery::applyFilters(<Model>::query()<with()>, $request, [<searchable>], [<sortable>], '<defaultSort>', '<defaultDir>');

        return TableExport::fromQuery($query, '<plural-kebab>', [<export columns: 'Heading' => 'accessor'>], $request->string('format', 'xlsx')->toString());
    }
}
```
When a task says a resource uses an Action (`Save<Model>::handle(...)`), `store`/`update` call the action instead of `create()`/`update()`.

**FormRequests** `app/Http/Requests/<Area>/Store<Model>Request.php` and `Update<Model>Request.php` — complete template:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\<Area>;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class Store<Model>Request extends FormRequest
{
    /** @var list<string> */
    protected array $booleans = [<boolean fields>];

    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true; // permission enforced by controller middleware
    }

    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    {
        foreach ($this->booleans as $field) {
            $this->merge([$field => $this->boolean($field)]);
        }
        <extra normalisation the task lists>
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [<rules, each as a PHP array of rule strings/objects>];
    }
}
```
`Update<Model>Request extends Store<Model>Request` and overrides `rules()` only where the task says (unique rules become `Rule::unique('<table>', '<col>')->ignore($this->route('<model>'))`).

**Index page** `resources/js/pages/<area>/<plural-kebab>/index.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Head, Link } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { ActiveBadge } from '@/components/erp/active-badge';
import { ConfirmDelete } from '@/components/erp/confirm-delete';
import { DataTable, type Column, type Paginated } from '@/components/erp/data-table';
import { ExportMenu } from '@/components/erp/export-menu';
import { PageHeader } from '@/components/erp/page-header';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useCan } from '@/lib/can';
import { create, destroy, edit, exportMethod, index } from '@/routes/<area>/<plural-kebab>';
import type { <Model> } from '@/types/<area>';

type Props = { rows: Paginated<<Model>>; filters: Record<string, string | undefined> };

export default function <Model>Index({ rows, filters }: Props) {
    const can = useCan();
    const columns: Column<<Model>>[] = [ <columns> ];
    return (
        <>
            <Head title="<Plural Spanish>" />
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-4">
                <PageHeader
                    title="<Plural Spanish>"
                    actions={<>
                        {can('<module>.<plural-kebab>.export') && <ExportMenu xlsxUrl={exportMethod.url({ query: { ...filters, format: 'xlsx' } })} csvUrl={exportMethod.url({ query: { ...filters, format: 'csv' } })} />}
                        {can('<module>.<plural-kebab>.create') && <Button asChild><Link href={create()}><Plus /> Nuevo <singular></Link></Button>}
                    </>}
                />
                <DataTable columns={columns} rows={rows} routeUrl={index.url()} filters={filters} searchPlaceholder="Buscar…"
                    rowActions={(row) => (<div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                        {can('<module>.<plural-kebab>.update') && <Button variant="outline" size="sm" asChild><Link href={edit(row.id)}>Editar</Link></Button>}
                        {can('<module>.<plural-kebab>.delete') && <ConfirmDelete url={destroy.url(row.id)} title="Eliminar <singular>" description="Esta acción no se puede deshacer." />}
                    </div>)} />
            </div>
        </>
    );
}
<Model>Index.layout = { breadcrumbs: [{ title: '<Plural Spanish>', href: index() }] };
```
Wayfinder renames the reserved word `export` to `exportMethod`. After `php artisan wayfinder:generate --with-form --no-interaction` run `grep -c 'export const exportMethod' resources/js/routes/<area>/<plural-kebab>/index.ts` → Expected `1`; if `0`, STOP and report the file's exported names.

**Form page** `resources/js/pages/<area>/<plural-kebab>/form.tsx` (one page for create and edit; `<model>` and `history` props optional). Uses Inertia's `<Form>` with Wayfinder form variants (`store.form()` / `update.form(id)`), uncontrolled inputs with `defaultValue`/`defaultChecked`, `<FormField>` per field, buttons "Guardar" (submit, `disabled={processing}`) and "Cancelar" (`<Link href={index()}>`). Checkboxes/switches submit `on`; the FormRequest's `prepareForValidation` normalises them; a **disabled** checkbox/switch is not submitted, so every form that disables one also renders `<input type="hidden" name="<field>" value="on" />` next to it. Selects use shadcn `Select` with `name` + `defaultValue`. Title: "Nuevo <singular>" / "Editar <singular>". Breadcrumbs: [`<Plural Spanish>` → index, title]. When editing (`history` present) the body is a shadcn `Tabs` (`defaultValue="datos"`) with `TabsTrigger` "Datos" (the form) and "Historial" (`<RecordHistory history={history} />`); when creating only the form renders.

**Factory** `database/factories/<Model>Factory.php` with the exact `definition()` the task gives.

**Feature tests.** Every CRUD resource registers the shared set with `crudResourceTests([...])` from `tests/Support/crud.php` (Task 8) — 10 tests: index forbidden / index ok / store forbidden / store validates required / store creates and logs / update changes and logs old+new / destroy deletes and logs / export xlsx / export logged / edit page includes history — followed by the task's own tests written in full. Payload helpers `valid<Model>Payload(array $over = []): array` live in `tests/Support/helpers.php` (each task appends its own; the file is in that task's Modify list).

### GC-8 Audit — everything leaves a trace (ARCH §6)
Every model created in this plan (master data, contacts, price list items, settings, import batches) uses `App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity` (Task 7). The only exemption is `ImportBatchRow` (bulk-inserted staging rows; the batch carries the audit trail). Custom events use `activity('<log_name>')->performedOn($m)->causedBy($u)->event('<snake_case>')->withProperties([...])->log('<Spanish description>')`. Exports are logged by `TableExport::fromQuery` (log `exports`), templates/imports by the import controller/service (log `imports`), auth by the auth controllers (log `auth`), role/permission changes by `LogAccessChanges` (log `access`). Audit rows are append-only (model guard + PostgreSQL trigger). Every task's tests assert the audit rows it writes.

### GC-9 Boost usage
Follow ARCH §9 (search-docs before unknown APIs; database-schema after migrate; last-error after failing requests). Boost is available from Task 3 on.

## Review Focus

Inputs the spec implies but which no single feature screen tests; each line names the owning task and the test added there.

1. **A Google account from the right domain whose user row exists but was deactivated after login** must be logged out on the next request, not just blocked at login → Task 4 `EnsureUserIsActive` test `inactive user is logged out on next request`.
2. **A super-admin trying to remove their own `super-admin` role or deactivate themselves** must be refused (otherwise support can lock itself out) → Task 9 tests `super admin cannot remove own super-admin role`, `user cannot deactivate self`.
3. **An exchange rate fetched for a date that already has a manual rate** must not overwrite the manual value (admin corrections win) → Task 13 test `scheduled fetch does not overwrite an existing manual rate`.
4. **An import file whose header row is missing or renamed** must be rejected at preview with a clear message, not create a batch of all-invalid rows → Task 24 test `preview rejects a file with missing required columns`.
5. **RNC/cédula with separators and spaces ("1-01-85004-3", "001-1391820-5")** must validate and be stored normalised (digits only) → Task 12 tests `rnc with dashes is normalised` / `cedula with dashes is normalised`, and Task 19 test `client tax id is stored normalised`.

### Task 2: PostgreSQL via Docker Compose, environment, Pest 5 on PostgreSQL

**Files:**
- Create: `docker-compose.yml`, `docker/postgres/init/01-create-test-db.sql`, `tests/Pest.php`, `tests/Support/helpers.php` (empty placeholder), `tests/Feature/SmokeTest.php`
- Modify: `.env`, `.env.example`, `phpunit.xml`, `config/app.php`, `composer.json` (dev deps)
- Delete: `database/database.sqlite`, `tests/Feature/Auth/`, `tests/Feature/Settings/`, `tests/Feature/DashboardTest.php`, `tests/Feature/ExampleTest.php`, `tests/Unit/ExampleTest.php`, `.github/`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: Docker service `db` (Postgres 17, port 5435); databases `gomez`, `gomez_test`; Pest 5 configured with `RefreshDatabase` on `tests/Feature`; `tests/Support/helpers.php` required by `tests/Pest.php`; `php artisan test` runs on PostgreSQL.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `docker-compose.yml`**

```yaml
services:
  db:
    image: postgres:17
    container_name: gomez-db
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:5435:5432"
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: gomez
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: gomez
      POSTGRES_DB: gomez
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./docker/postgres/init:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d:ro
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U gomez -d gomez"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20

volumes:
  db-data:
```

**Interfaces:**

This task deliberately overrides ARCH §10's never-commit list: `.mcp.json`, `boost.json`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/` and `.ai/` **are committed** so headless executors get the MCP configuration (ARCH §10 was amended to say the same).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `CLAUDE.md` (Boost will append its block below this content)**

```markdown
# Gomez ERP

ERP for Gomez International (Dominican Republic). Laravel 13 + Inertia 3 + React 19 + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui, PostgreSQL 17 (Docker), Pest 5.

- Code, identifiers, routes and commits in English. All user-facing text in Spanish (es-DO).
- Money: `decimal` columns only, computed with `App\Support\Money\Decimal` (brick/math, HALF_UP). Never `float`.
- Plans that govern all work: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-00-architecture.md` (conventions) and `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-0N-*.md` (phase tasks). Follow the task you were given literally.
- Database: `docker compose up -d db` (port 5435). Tests: `php artisan test` (PostgreSQL `gomez_test`), full gate: `composer test`.
- Frontend: `npm run dev`, `npm run build`, `npm run check:fix`, `npm run types:check`. Routes in TS come from Wayfinder (`@/routes/...`).
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the migration `database/migrations/2026_10_29_000010_add_google_login_fields_to_users_table.php`**

```php
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->string('password')->nullable()->change();
            $table->string('google_id', 64)->nullable()->unique()->after('email');
            $table->string('avatar_url', 500)->nullable()->after('google_id');
            $table->boolean('is_active')->default(true)->after('avatar_url');
            $table->timestamp('last_login_at')->nullable()->after('is_active');
        });
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        Schema::table('users', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropColumn(['google_id', 'avatar_url', 'is_active', 'last_login_at']);
            $table->string('password')->nullable(false)->change();
        });
    }
};
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\UserFactory> */
    use HasFactory, Notifiable;

    protected $fillable = ['name', 'email', 'google_id', 'avatar_url', 'is_active', 'last_login_at'];

    protected $hidden = ['password', 'remember_token'];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
            'last_login_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
        ];
    }
}
```

`database/factories/UserFactory.php` (full replacement):
```php
<?php

namespace Database\Factories;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/** @extends Factory<User> */
class UserFactory extends Factory
{
    protected $model = User::class;

    public function definition(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => fake()->name(),
            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
            'google_id' => null,
            'avatar_url' => null,
            'is_active' => true,
            'last_login_at' => null,
            'password' => null,
            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
        ];
    }

    public function inactive(): static
    {
        return $this->state(fn () => ['is_active' => false]);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Write `app/Http/Middleware/EnsureUserIsActive.php` and register it**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class EnsureUserIsActive
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if ($user !== null && ! $user->is_active) {
            Auth::guard('web')->logout();
            $request->session()->invalidate();
            $request->session()->regenerateToken();

            return redirect()->route('login')->with('error', 'Tu cuenta está desactivada. Contacta a un administrador.');
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}
```
In `bootstrap/app.php` add `EnsureUserIsActive::class` to the `$middleware->web(append: [...])` array **before** `HandleInertiaRequests::class` (add the `use App\Http\Middleware\EnsureUserIsActive;` import).

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `app/Support/PermissionCatalog.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Support;

final class PermissionCatalog
{
    public const SUPER_ADMIN = 'super-admin';

    /** @var array<string, string> role name => Spanish label */
    public const ROLES = [
        'super-admin' => 'Super Admin',
        'owner' => 'Dueña',
        'management' => 'Gerencia',
        'sales' => 'Ventas',
        'accounting' => 'Contabilidad',
        'warehouse' => 'Almacén',
    ];

    /** Roles that receive every non-admin permission by default. */
    private const BUSINESS_ALL = ['owner', 'management', 'accounting'];

    /** Roles allowed to export tables by default (spec: warehouse never exports). */
    private const EXPORTERS = ['owner', 'management', 'accounting', 'sales'];

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    public static function modules(): array
    {
        return [
            'admin' => 'Administración',
            'catalog' => 'Catálogo',
            'finance' => 'Finanzas',
            'inventory' => 'Inventario',
        ];
    }

    /** @return array<string, array{label: string, module: string, roles: list<string>}> */
    public static function permissions(): array
    {
        $p = [];
        $add = function (string $name, string $label, array $roles) use (&$p): void {
            $p[$name] = ['label' => $label, 'module' => explode('.', $name)[0], 'roles' => array_values(array_unique($roles))];
        };
        $crud = function (string $prefix, string $plural, array $viewRoles, array $writeRoles, bool $import = false) use ($add): void {
            $add("$prefix.view", "Ver $plural", $viewRoles);
            $add("$prefix.create", "Crear $plural", $writeRoles);
            $add("$prefix.update", "Editar $plural", $writeRoles);
            $add("$prefix.delete", "Eliminar $plural", array_values(array_diff($writeRoles, ['sales'])));
            $add("$prefix.export", "Exportar $plural", array_values(array_intersect($viewRoles, self::EXPORTERS)));
            if ($import) {
                $add("$prefix.import", "Importar $plural", array_values(array_diff($writeRoles, ['sales'])));
            }
        };

        $all = self::BUSINESS_ALL;

        // admin — spec §2: Gerencia gets nothing under admin.*
        $add('admin.users.view', 'Ver usuarios', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.users.create', 'Invitar usuarios', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.users.update', 'Editar usuarios', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.users.deactivate', 'Desactivar usuarios', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.users.impersonate', 'Entrar como otro usuario', []);
        $add('admin.roles.view', 'Ver roles', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.roles.update', 'Editar permisos de roles', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.settings.view', 'Ver configuración', ['owner', 'accounting']);
        $add('admin.settings.manage', 'Editar configuración', ['owner']);
        $add('admin.activity-log.view', 'Ver bitácora', ['owner', 'accounting']);
        $add('admin.system.manage', 'Administrar sistema (técnico)', []);

        // catalog
        $crud('catalog.clients', 'clientes', [...$all, 'sales'], [...$all, 'sales'], true);
        $add('catalog.clients.view-all', 'Ver clientes de todos los vendedores', $all);
        $crud('catalog.suppliers', 'proveedores', $all, $all, true);
        $crud('catalog.brands', 'marcas', [...$all, 'sales', 'warehouse'], $all);
        $crud('catalog.categories', 'categorías', [...$all, 'sales', 'warehouse'], $all);
        $crud('catalog.units', 'unidades de medida', [...$all, 'warehouse'], $all);
        $crud('catalog.products', 'productos', [...$all, 'sales', 'warehouse'], $all, true);
        $add('catalog.products.view-costs', 'Ver costos de productos', $all);
        $crud('catalog.price-lists', 'listas de precios', [...$all, 'sales'], $all, true);
        $crud('catalog.salespeople', 'vendedores', [...$all, 'sales'], $all);
        $crud('catalog.additional-taxes', 'impuestos adicionales', $all, $all);
        $add('catalog.tax-ids.override-validation', 'Forzar RNC/cédula que no pasa la validación', ['owner']);

        // finance
        $add('finance.currencies.manage', 'Administrar monedas', $all);
        $add('finance.exchange-rates.view', 'Ver tasas de cambio', [...$all, 'sales']);
        $add('finance.exchange-rates.manage', 'Registrar tasas de cambio', $all);
        $add('finance.accounts.view', 'Ver cuentas bancarias y cajas', $all);
        $add('finance.accounts.manage', 'Administrar cuentas bancarias y cajas', $all);

        // inventory (phase 1 subset)
        $add('inventory.warehouses.view', 'Ver almacenes', [...$all, 'warehouse']);
        $add('inventory.warehouses.manage', 'Administrar almacenes', ['owner', 'management']);

        return $p;
    }

    /** @return list<string> */
    public static function names(): array
    {
        return array_keys(self::permissions());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `database/seeders/PermissionSeeder.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace Database\Seeders;

use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class PermissionSeeder extends Seeder
{
    public function run(): void
    {
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

        $roles = [];
        $newRoles = [];
        foreach (array_keys(PermissionCatalog::ROLES) as $name) {
            $role = Role::firstOrCreate(['name' => $name, 'guard_name' => 'web']);
            $roles[$name] = $role;
            if ($role->wasRecentlyCreated) {
                $newRoles[] = $name;
            }
        }

        foreach (PermissionCatalog::permissions() as $name => $def) {
            $permission = Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => $name, 'guard_name' => 'web']);
            foreach ($def['roles'] as $roleName) {
                // Grant defaults when the permission is new or the role is new; never revoke admin edits.
                if ($permission->wasRecentlyCreated || in_array($roleName, $newRoles, true)) {
                    $roles[$roleName]->givePermissionTo($permission);
                }
            }
        }

        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `app/Actions/Admin/SyncSuperAdmins.php`, `database/seeders/SuperAdminSeeder.php`, update `DatabaseSeeder`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Admin;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class SyncSuperAdmins
{
    /** Ensures every email in config('erp.super_admin_emails') exists as an active super-admin. Returns the number of users created. */
    public function handle(): int
    {
        $created = 0;
        foreach (config('erp.super_admin_emails', []) as $email) {
            $user = User::query()->whereRaw('lower(email) = ?', [$email])->first();
            if ($user === null) {
                $user = User::create(['name' => Str::before($email, '@'), 'email' => $email, 'is_active' => true]);
                $created++;
            }
            if (! $user->hasRole(PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN)) {
                $user->assignRole(PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN);
            }
        }

        return $created;
    }
}
```

```php
    }
}
```

Replace `HandleInertiaRequests::share()` with:
```php
public function share(Request $request): array
{
    $user = $request->user();
    $impersonatorId = $request->session()->get('impersonator_id');
    $impersonator = $impersonatorId ? \App\Models\User::query()->find($impersonatorId) : null;

    return [
        ...parent::share($request),
        'name' => config('app.name'),
        'auth' => [
            'user' => $user ? $user->only(['id', 'name', 'email', 'avatar_url', 'is_active', 'last_login_at', 'created_at', 'updated_at']) : null,
            'roles' => $user ? $user->getRoleNames()->values()->all() : [],
            'permissions' => $user ? $user->getAllPermissions()->pluck('name')->values()->all() : [],
            'impersonator' => $impersonator ? ['id' => $impersonator->id, 'name' => $impersonator->name] : null,
        ],
        'flash' => [
            'success' => fn () => $request->session()->get('success'),
            'error' => fn () => $request->session()->get('error'),
        ],
        'sidebarOpen' => ! $request->hasCookie('sidebar_state') || $request->cookie('sidebar_state') === 'true',
    ];
}
```
Write `resources/js/lib/can.ts`:
```ts
import { usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';

export function useCan(): (permission: string) => boolean {
    const { auth } = usePage().props;
    return (permission: string) =>
        auth.roles.includes('super-admin') || auth.permissions.includes(permission);
}
```

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;

function actingAsRole(string $role, array $attributes = []): User
{
```

```php
<?php

use App\Actions\Admin\SyncSuperAdmins;

test('seeder creates all roles and permissions idempotently', function () {
    (new PermissionSeeder)->run();
    (new PermissionSeeder)->run();

    expect(Role::count())->toBe(6)
        ->and(Permission::count())->toBe(count(PermissionCatalog::names()))
        ->and(Role::findByName('owner')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.clients.create'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Role::findByName('sales')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.clients.view-all'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Role::findByName('sales')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.clients.export'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Role::findByName('warehouse')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.products.view'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Role::findByName('warehouse')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.products.export'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Role::findByName('accounting')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.salespeople.create'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Role::findByName('owner')->hasPermissionTo('admin.system.manage'))->toBeFalse()
        ->and(Role::findByName('owner')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.tax-ids.override-validation'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(Role::findByName('accounting')->hasPermissionTo('catalog.tax-ids.override-validation'))->toBeFalse();
});

});

        ->and(User::where('email', 'soporte@externo.test')->first()->hasRole('super-admin'))->toBeTrue()
        ->and(app(SyncSuperAdmins::class)->handle())->toBe(0);
});

test('shared props expose roles and permissions', function () {

        ->where('auth.roles', ['sales'])
        ->where('auth.impersonator', null)

});
```

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use Database\Seeders\PermissionSeeder;
use Laravel\Socialite\Facades\Socialite;
use Laravel\Socialite\Two\User as GoogleUser;

beforeEach(function () {
    (new PermissionSeeder)->run();
    config(['erp.google_workspace_domain' => 'empresa.test', 'erp.super_admin_emails' => ['soporte@externo.test']]);
});

function fakeGoogle(array $overrides = []): void
{
    Socialite::fake('google', GoogleUser::fake(array_merge([
        'id' => 'google-123', 'name' => 'Ana Pérez', 'email' => 'ana@empresa.test',
        'avatar' => 'https://example.test/a.png', 'hd' => 'empresa.test', 'email_verified' => true,
    ], $overrides)));
}

function activityInstalled(): bool
{
    return class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class);
}

test('login page renders the google button', function () {
    $this->get(route('login'))->assertOk()->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->component('auth/login'));
});

test('redirect route sends the user to google', function () {
    Socialite::fake('google');
    $this->get(route('auth.google.redirect'))->assertRedirect();
});

test('invited active user from the workspace domain logs in', function () {
    $user = User::factory()->create(['email' => 'ana@empresa.test']);
    fakeGoogle();

    $this->get(route('auth.google.callback'))->assertRedirect(route('dashboard'));

    $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($user);
    $user->refresh();
    expect($user->google_id)->toBe('google-123')
        ->and($user->avatar_url)->toBe('https://example.test/a.png')
        ->and($user->last_login_at)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::inLog('auth')->where('event', 'login')->where('causer_id', $user->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();
})->skip(! activityInstalled(), 'activitylog is installed in Task 7');

test('email matching is case insensitive', function () {
    $user = User::factory()->create(['email' => 'ana@empresa.test']);
    fakeGoogle(['email' => 'Ana@EMPRESA.TEST']);

    $this->get(route('auth.google.callback'))->assertRedirect(route('dashboard'));
    $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($user);
});

test('account outside the workspace domain is rejected', function () {
    User::factory()->create(['email' => 'ana@otro.test']);
    fakeGoogle(['email' => 'ana@otro.test', 'hd' => 'otro.test']);

    $this->get(route('auth.google.callback'))
        ->assertRedirect(route('login'))
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Exceptions;

use RuntimeException;

class LoginRejectedException extends RuntimeException
{
    public function __construct(string $message, public readonly string $email, public readonly string $reason)
    {
        parent::__construct($message);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `app/Actions/Auth/LoginWithGoogle.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Auth;

use App\Actions\Admin\SyncSuperAdmins;
use App\Exceptions\LoginRejectedException;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;
use Laravel\Socialite\Contracts\User as GoogleUser;

class LoginWithGoogle
{
    public function __construct(private readonly SyncSuperAdmins $syncSuperAdmins) {}

    public function handle(GoogleUser $googleUser): User
    {
        $raw = $googleUser->getRaw();
        $email = strtolower(trim((string) $googleUser->getEmail()));
        $domain = (string) config('erp.google_workspace_domain');
        $isSupport = in_array($email, config('erp.super_admin_emails', []), true);

        if (($raw['email_verified'] ?? false) !== true) {
            throw new LoginRejectedException('Google no ha verificado el correo de esta cuenta.', $email, 'unverified');
        }

        if (! $isSupport) {
            $hd = $raw['hd'] ?? null;
            if ($hd === null) {
                throw new LoginRejectedException("Solo se permite iniciar sesión con una cuenta de Google Workspace de {$domain}.", $email, 'hd_missing');
            }
            if ($domain === '' || $hd !== $domain) {
                throw new LoginRejectedException("Solo se permite iniciar sesión con una cuenta de Google Workspace de {$domain}.", $email, 'domain');
            }
        }

        if ($isSupport) {
            $this->syncSuperAdmins->handle();
        }

        $user = User::query()->whereRaw('lower(email) = ?', [$email])->first();

        if ($user === null) {
            throw new LoginRejectedException('Tu cuenta no está registrada en el sistema. Pide a un administrador que te invite.', $email, 'not_invited');
        }

        if (! $user->is_active) {
            throw new LoginRejectedException('Tu cuenta está desactivada. Contacta a un administrador.', $email, 'inactive');
        }

        $user->forceFill([
            'google_id' => (string) $googleUser->getId(),
            'avatar_url' => $googleUser->getAvatar(),
            'last_login_at' => now(),
        ])->save();

        Auth::guard('web')->login($user, remember: true);

        return $user;
    }
}
```

`app/Http/Controllers/Auth/GoogleLoginController.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Actions\Auth\LoginWithGoogle;
use App\Exceptions\LoginRejectedException;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Laravel\Socialite\Facades\Socialite;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\RedirectResponse as SymfonyRedirect;
use Throwable;

class GoogleLoginController extends Controller
{
    public function redirect(): SymfonyRedirect
    {
        $params = ['prompt' => 'select_account'];
        if ($domain = config('erp.google_workspace_domain')) {
            $params['hd'] = $domain;
        }

        return Socialite::driver('google')->with($params)->redirect();
    }

    public function callback(Request $request, LoginWithGoogle $action): RedirectResponse
    {
        try {
            $googleUser = Socialite::driver('google')->user();
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            if (class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class)) {
                activity('auth')->event('login_rejected')->withProperties(['email' => null, 'reason' => 'oauth_error', 'ip' => $request->ip(), 'message' => $e->getMessage()])->log('Inicio de sesión rechazado');
            }

            return redirect()->route('login')->with('error', 'No se pudo completar el inicio de sesión con Google. Inténtalo de nuevo.');
        }

        try {
            $user = $action->handle($googleUser);
        } catch (LoginRejectedException $e) {
            if (class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class)) {
                activity('auth')->event('login_rejected')->withProperties(['email' => $e->email, 'reason' => $e->reason, 'ip' => $request->ip()])->log('Inicio de sesión rechazado');
            }

            return redirect()->route('login')->with('error', $e->getMessage());
        }

        $request->session()->regenerate();
        if (class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class)) {
            activity('auth')->causedBy($user)->event('login')->withProperties(['ip' => $request->ip()])->log('Inicio de sesión');
        }

        return redirect()->intended(route('dashboard'));
    }
}
```
`app/Http/Controllers/Auth/LogoutController.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Auth;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class LogoutController extends Controller
{
    public function __invoke(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        if (class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class) && $request->user()) {
            activity('auth')->causedBy($request->user())->event('logout')->withProperties(['ip' => $request->ip()])->log('Cierre de sesión');
        }
        Auth::guard('web')->logout();
        $request->session()->invalidate();
        $request->session()->regenerateToken();

        return redirect()->route('login');
    }
}
```

### Task 7: Append-only activity log with request context, model descriptions, role/permission events

**Files:**
- Create: `app/Models/Concerns/LogsModelActivity.php`, `app/Models/ActivityLog.php`, `app/Exceptions/AppendOnlyAuditException.php`, `app/Http/Middleware/AssignRequestId.php`, `app/Listeners/LogAccessChanges.php`, `database/migrations/2026_10_29_000005_create_activity_log_table.php` (published and renamed), `database/migrations/2026_10_29_000011_add_context_and_append_only_guard_to_activity_log.php`, `tests/Feature/Admin/ActivityLogTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: trait `App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity` (ARCH §6.1 options; description "Registro creado/actualizado/eliminado", or "Registro activado/desactivado" when `is_active` changed); `App\Models\ActivityLog` (configured `activity_model`; relation `impersonator()`; throws `AppendOnlyAuditException` on update/delete); `activity_log` columns `impersonator_id`, `ip`, `user_agent`, `request_id` filled automatically by the `ActivityLog::creating` hook; PostgreSQL trigger `activity_log_append_only`; middleware `AssignRequestId` (global; binds `request.id` in the container; header `X-Request-Id`); listener `LogAccessChanges` writing log `access` events `role_assigned`, `role_removed`, `permission_granted`, `permission_revoked` (properties `names`); `User` uses `CausesActivity` + `LogsModelActivity`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the context + guard migration `database/migrations/2026_10_29_000011_add_context_and_append_only_guard_to_activity_log.php`**

```php
<?php

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

return new class extends Migration
{
    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::table('activity_log', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->foreignId('impersonator_id')->nullable()->after('causer_id')->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete();
            $table->string('ip', 45)->nullable()->after('properties');
            $table->string('user_agent', 500)->nullable()->after('ip');
            $table->string('request_id', 36)->nullable()->after('user_agent')->index();
        });

        DB::unprepared(<<<'SQL'
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_activity_log_mutation() RETURNS trigger AS $$
            BEGIN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'activity_log is append-only';
            END;
            $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

            CREATE TRIGGER activity_log_append_only
                BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON activity_log
                FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_activity_log_mutation();
        SQL);
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        DB::unprepared('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS activity_log_append_only ON activity_log; DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS prevent_activity_log_mutation();');
        Schema::table('activity_log', function (Blueprint $table) {
            $table->dropConstrainedForeignId('impersonator_id');
            $table->dropColumn(['ip', 'user_agent', 'request_id']);
        });
    }
};
```

`app/Models/Concerns/LogsModelActivity.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Models\Concerns;

use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Concerns\LogsActivity;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Support\LogOptions;

trait LogsModelActivity
{
    use LogsActivity;

    public function getActivitylogOptions(): LogOptions
    {
        return LogOptions::defaults()
            ->logOnly(['*'])
            ->logOnlyDirty()
            ->dontLogEmptyChanges()
            ->useLogName($this->getTable())
            ->setDescriptionForEvent(fn (string $event): string => $this->auditDescription($event));
    }

    protected function auditDescription(string $event): string
    {
        if ($event === 'updated' && $this->wasChanged('is_active')) {
            return $this->is_active ? 'Registro activado' : 'Registro desactivado';
        }

        return match ($event) {
            'created' => 'Registro creado',
            'updated' => 'Registro actualizado',
            'deleted' => 'Registro eliminado',
            default => "Registro {$event}",
        };
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Request id middleware, the `creating` hook, guard removal**

`app/Http/Middleware/AssignRequestId.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

class AssignRequestId
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $id = (string) Str::uuid();
        app()->instance('request.id', $id);
        $response = $next($request);
        $response->headers->set('X-Request-Id', $id);

        return $response;
    }
}
```
`bootstrap/app.php`: inside `withMiddleware` add `$middleware->prepend(AssignRequestId::class);` (import `App\Http\Middleware\AssignRequestId`).
`app/Providers/AppServiceProvider.php::boot()` — add (imports `App\Models\ActivityLog`, `Illuminate\Support\Str`):
```php
ActivityLog::creating(function (ActivityLog $activity): void {
    // `request.id` is bound only by AssignRequestId (HTTP); console and queue work have none.
    if (! app()->bound('request.id')) {
        $activity->user_agent = 'console';

        return;
    }
    $request = request();
    $activity->request_id = app('request.id');
    $activity->ip = $request->ip();
    $activity->user_agent = Str::limit((string) $request->userAgent(), 500, '');
    if ($request->hasSession() && ($impersonatorId = $request->session()->get('impersonator_id'))) {
        $activity->impersonator_id = $impersonatorId;
    }
});
```
Remove every `if (class_exists(\Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity::class) …)` wrapper: three in `GoogleLoginController::callback` (keep the inner `activity('auth')…` lines unconditional) and one in `LogoutController::__invoke` (it becomes `if ($request->user()) { activity('auth')… }`).
Run: `grep -rn 'class_exists' app/Http/Controllers/Auth; echo "exit=$?"` → Expected: no lines, `exit=1`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Role/permission event listener `app/Listeners/LogAccessChanges.php`**

Spatie 8.3.2 event payloads (verified in `vendor/spatie/laravel-permission/src/Events/*Event.php`): `RoleAttachedEvent`/`RoleDetachedEvent` have `public Model $model, public mixed $rolesOrIds`; `PermissionAttachedEvent`/`PermissionDetachedEvent` have `public Model $model, public mixed $permissionsOrIds`. The payload is whatever was passed to `assignRole()`/`givePermissionTo()`: strings, ids, model instances, or arrays/collections of them.
```php
<?php

namespace App\Listeners;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Spatie\Permission\Events\PermissionAttachedEvent;
use Spatie\Permission\Events\PermissionDetachedEvent;
use Spatie\Permission\Events\RoleAttachedEvent;
use Spatie\Permission\Events\RoleDetachedEvent;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;

class LogAccessChanges
{
    public function handle(RoleAttachedEvent|RoleDetachedEvent|PermissionAttachedEvent|PermissionDetachedEvent $event): void
    {
        [$verb, $label, $isRole] = match ($event::class) {
            RoleAttachedEvent::class => ['role_assigned', 'Rol asignado', true],
            RoleDetachedEvent::class => ['role_removed', 'Rol quitado', true],
            PermissionAttachedEvent::class => ['permission_granted', 'Permiso otorgado', false],
            PermissionDetachedEvent::class => ['permission_revoked', 'Permiso revocado', false],
        };

        $given = $isRole ? $event->rolesOrIds : $event->permissionsOrIds;
        $names = collect(is_iterable($given) ? $given : [$given])
            ->flatten()
            ->map(function (mixed $item) use ($isRole): string {
                if ($item instanceof Model) {
                    return (string) $item->getAttribute('name');
                }
                if (is_numeric($item)) {
                    $model = $isRole ? Role::query()->find((int) $item) : Permission::query()->find((int) $item);

                    return $model ? (string) $model->name : (string) $item;
                }

                return (string) $item;
            })
            ->unique()->values()->all();

        activity('access')
            ->performedOn($event->model)
            ->event($verb)
            ->withProperties(['names' => $names])
            ->log($label.': '.implode(', ', $names));
    }
}
```
Register in `AppServiceProvider::boot()` (imports `Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event`, the four event classes, `App\Listeners\LogAccessChanges`):
```php
Event::listen([RoleAttachedEvent::class, RoleDetachedEvent::class, PermissionAttachedEvent::class, PermissionDetachedEvent::class], LogAccessChanges::class);
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write `tests/Feature/Admin/ActivityLogTest.php`**

```php
<?php

use App\Exceptions\AppendOnlyAuditException;
use App\Models\ActivityLog;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

test('model changes are logged with old and new values and request context', function () {
    $actor = actingAsRole('owner');
    $user = User::factory()->create(['name' => 'Antes']);
    $this->withHeader('User-Agent', 'PestAgent/1.0')->get(route('dashboard'))->assertOk();
    $user->update(['name' => 'Después']);

    $activity = Activity::inLog('users')->where('event', 'updated')->where('subject_id', $user->id)->latest('id')->firstOrFail();

    expect($activity->causer_id)->toBe($actor->id)
        ->and($activity->description)->toBe('Registro actualizado')
        ->and($activity->attribute_changes['attributes']['name'])->toBe('Después')
        ->and($activity->attribute_changes['old']['name'])->toBe('Antes')
        ->and(array_key_exists('updated_at', $activity->attribute_changes['attributes']))->toBeFalse()
        ->and($activity->request_id)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($activity->ip)->toBe('127.0.0.1')
        ->and($activity->user_agent)->toBe('PestAgent/1.0');
});

test('deactivation gets an explicit description', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $user = User::factory()->create();
    $user->update(['is_active' => false]);

    expect(Activity::inLog('users')->where('subject_id', $user->id)->latest('id')->first()->description)->toBe('Registro desactivado');
});

test('activities created while impersonating carry the impersonator id', function () {
    $admin = actingAsRole('super-admin');
    $target = User::factory()->create();

    $this->actingAs($target)->withSession(['impersonator_id' => $admin->id])->get(route('dashboard'))
        ->assertOk()
        ->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->where('auth.impersonator.id', $admin->id));

    session(['impersonator_id' => $admin->id]);
    activity('test')->causedBy($target)->log('prueba');

    expect(Activity::inLog('test')->latest('id')->first()->impersonator_id)->toBe($admin->id);
});

test('audit rows cannot be updated or deleted by the model', function () {
    activity('test')->log('x');
    $row = ActivityLog::latest('id')->firstOrFail();

    expect(fn () => $row->update(['description' => 'y']))->toThrow(AppendOnlyAuditException::class);
    expect(fn () => $row->delete())->toThrow(AppendOnlyAuditException::class);
});

test('audit rows cannot be updated or deleted at the database level', function () {
    activity('test')->log('x');
    $id = Activity::latest('id')->firstOrFail()->id;

    expect(fn () => DB::table('activity_log')->where('id', $id)->update(['description' => 'y']))->toThrow(QueryException::class, 'activity_log is append-only');
    expect(fn () => DB::table('activity_log')->where('id', $id)->delete())->toThrow(QueryException::class, 'activity_log is append-only');
});

test('role assignment and removal are logged', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $user = User::factory()->create();
    $user->assignRole('sales');
    $user->removeRole('sales');

    $assigned = Activity::inLog('access')->where('event', 'role_assigned')->where('subject_id', $user->id)->latest('id')->first();
    $removed = Activity::inLog('access')->where('event', 'role_removed')->where('subject_id', $user->id)->latest('id')->first();

    expect($assigned)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($assigned->properties['names'])->toBe(['sales'])
        ->and($assigned->description)->toBe('Rol asignado: sales')
        ->and($removed)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($removed->properties['names'])->toBe(['sales']);
});

test('responses carry a request id header', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->get(route('dashboard'))->assertHeader('X-Request-Id');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `resources/js/lib/format.ts`**

```ts
export function formatMoney(value: string | number | null | undefined, currency: 'DOP' | 'USD' = 'DOP'): string {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return '';
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-DO', { style: 'currency', currency, minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }).format(Number(value));
}

export function formatNumber(value: string | number | null | undefined, decimals = 2): string {
    if (value === null || value === undefined || value === '') return '';
    return new Intl.NumberFormat('es-DO', { minimumFractionDigits: decimals, maximumFractionDigits: decimals }).format(Number(value));
}

export function formatDate(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
    if (!iso) return '';
    const d = new Date(iso);
    return `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/${d.getFullYear()}`;
}

export function formatDateTime(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
    if (!iso) return '';
    const d = new Date(iso);
    return `${formatDate(iso)} ${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
```
(Display only — `Number()` is acceptable here because the frontend never computes money; it formats server strings.)

```ts
import { usePage } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export function useFlashMessages(): void {
    const { flash } = usePage().props;

    useEffect(() => {
        if (flash.success) toast.success(flash.success);
        if (flash.error) toast.error(flash.error);
    }, [flash.success, flash.error]);
}
```

```tsx
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/format';
import type { AuditEntry } from '@/types/audit';

const show = (v: unknown): string => (v === null || v === undefined || v === '' ? '—' : typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v));

export function RecordHistory({ history }: { history: AuditEntry[] }) {
    if (history.length === 0) {
        return <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sin historial</p>;
    }
    return (
        <div className="rounded-md border">
            <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                    <TableRow>
                        <TableHead>Fecha</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>Usuario</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>Evento</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>Descripción</TableHead>
                        <TableHead>Cambios</TableHead>
                    </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                    {history.map((h) => (
                        <TableRow key={h.id}>
                            <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap">{formatDateTime(h.created_at)}</TableCell>
                            <TableCell>
                                {h.causer ?? 'Sistema'}
                                {h.impersonator && <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">suplantado por {h.impersonator}</span>}
                            </TableCell>
                            <TableCell>{h.event ?? '—'}</TableCell>
                            <TableCell>{h.description}</TableCell>
                            <TableCell>
                                {h.changes.length === 0 ? '—' : (
                                    <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs">
                                        {h.changes.map((c) => (
                                            <li key={c.field}><span className="font-medium">{c.field}</span>: {show(c.old)} → {show(c.new)}</li>
                                        ))}
                                    </ul>
                                )}
                            </TableCell>
                        </TableRow>
                    ))}
                </TableBody>
            </Table>
        </div>
    );
}
```

`resources/js/components/erp/data-table.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link, router } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, ArrowUpDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { type ReactNode, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export type Column<Row> = { key: string; label: string; sortable?: boolean; className?: string; render?: (row: Row) => ReactNode };

export type Paginated<Row> = {
    data: Row[]; current_page: number; last_page: number; per_page: number; total: number;
    from: number | null; to: number | null; links: { url: string | null; label: string; active: boolean }[];
};

export type DataTableProps<Row extends { id: number }> = {
    columns: Column<Row>[]; rows: Paginated<Row>; routeUrl: string;
    filters: Record<string, string | undefined>;
    searchPlaceholder?: string; emptyText?: string;
    rowActions?: (row: Row) => ReactNode; toolbar?: ReactNode;
};

export function DataTable<Row extends { id: number }>({ columns, rows, routeUrl, filters, searchPlaceholder = 'Buscar…', emptyText = 'No hay registros', rowActions, toolbar }: DataTableProps<Row>) {
    const [search, setSearch] = useState(filters.search ?? '');

    const visit = (params: Record<string, string | undefined>) =>
        router.get(routeUrl, { ...filters, ...params }, { preserveState: true, preserveScroll: true, replace: true });

    useEffect(() => {
        if ((filters.search ?? '') === search) return;
        const t = setTimeout(() => visit({ search: search || undefined, page: undefined }), 300);
        return () => clearTimeout(t);
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [search]);

    const toggleSort = (key: string) => {
        const dir = filters.sort === key && filters.dir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        visit({ sort: key, dir, page: undefined });
    };

    return (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                {toolbar}
                <Input className="max-w-xs" placeholder={searchPlaceholder} value={search} onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)} />
                <Select value={String(rows.per_page)} onValueChange={(v) => visit({ per_page: v, page: undefined })}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="w-24"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                        {['15', '25', '50', '100'].map((n) => <SelectItem key={n} value={n}>{n}</SelectItem>)}
                    </SelectContent>
                </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-md border">
                <Table>
                    <TableHeader>
                        <TableRow>
                            {columns.map((c) => (
                                <TableHead key={c.key} className={c.className}>
                                    {c.sortable ? (
                                        <button type="button" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1" onClick={() => toggleSort(c.key)}>
                                            {c.label}
                                            {filters.sort === c.key ? (filters.dir === 'asc' ? <ArrowUp className="size-3" /> : <ArrowDown className="size-3" />) : <ArrowUpDown className="size-3 opacity-40" />}
                                        </button>
                                    ) : c.label}
                                </TableHead>
                            ))}
                            {rowActions && <TableHead className="w-0 text-right">Acciones</TableHead>}
                        </TableRow>
                    </TableHeader>
                    <TableBody>
                        {rows.data.length === 0 && (
                            <TableRow><TableCell colSpan={columns.length + (rowActions ? 1 : 0)} className="py-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">{emptyText}</TableCell></TableRow>
                        )}
                        {rows.data.map((row) => (
                            <TableRow key={row.id}>
                                {columns.map((c) => (
                                    <TableCell key={c.key} className={c.className}>
                                        {c.render ? c.render(row) : String((row as Record<string, unknown>)[c.key] ?? '')}
                                    </TableCell>
                                ))}
                                {rowActions && <TableCell className="text-right">{rowActions(row)}</TableCell>}
                            </TableRow>
                        ))}
                    </TableBody>
                </Table>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <span>{rows.total === 0 ? 'Sin resultados' : `Mostrando ${rows.from}–${rows.to} de ${rows.total}`}</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                    {rows.links.map((l, i) => (
                        <Button key={i} asChild={!!l.url} variant={l.active ? 'default' : 'outline'} size="sm" disabled={!l.url} className={cn(!l.url && 'opacity-50')}>
                            {l.url ? <Link href={l.url} preserveState preserveScroll><span dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: l.label }} /></Link> : <span dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: l.label }} />}
                        </Button>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link } from '@inertiajs/react';
import { SidebarGroup, SidebarGroupLabel, SidebarMenu, SidebarMenuButton, SidebarMenuItem } from '@/components/ui/sidebar';
import { useCurrentUrl } from '@/hooks/use-current-url';
import { useCan } from '@/lib/can';
import type { NavGroup } from '@/nav';

export function NavMain({ groups }: { groups: NavGroup[] }) {
    const { isCurrentUrl } = useCurrentUrl();
    const can = useCan();

    return (
        <>
            {groups.map((group) => {
                const items = group.items.filter((item) => !item.permission || can(item.permission));
                if (items.length === 0) return null;
                return (
                    <SidebarGroup key={group.label} className="px-2 py-0">
                        <SidebarGroupLabel>{group.label}</SidebarGroupLabel>
                        <SidebarMenu>
                            {items.map((item) => (
                                <SidebarMenuItem key={item.title}>
                                    <SidebarMenuButton asChild isActive={isCurrentUrl(item.href)} tooltip={{ children: item.title }}>
                                        <Link href={item.href} prefetch>
                                            {item.icon && <item.icon />}
                                            <span>{item.title}</span>
                                        </Link>
                                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                                </SidebarMenuItem>
                            ))}
                        </SidebarMenu>
                    </SidebarGroup>
                );
            })}
        </>
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `app/Support/TableQuery.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Support;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Pagination\LengthAwarePaginator;

final class TableQuery
{
    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $searchable
     * @param  list<string>  $sortable
     */
    public static function applyFilters(Builder $query, Request $request, array $searchable, array $sortable, string $defaultSort = 'id', string $defaultDir = 'desc'): Builder
    {
        $search = trim((string) $request->query('search', ''));
        if ($search !== '' && $searchable !== []) {
            $query->where(function (Builder $q) use ($search, $searchable): void {
                foreach ($searchable as $column) {
                    $q->orWhere($column, 'ILIKE', '%'.str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\%', '\_'], $search).'%');
                }
            });
        }

        $sort = (string) $request->query('sort', $defaultSort);
        $dir = strtolower((string) $request->query('dir', $defaultDir)) === 'asc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
        if (! in_array($sort, $sortable, true)) {
            $sort = $defaultSort;
        }

        return $query->orderBy($sort, $dir)->orderBy($query->getModel()->getQualifiedKeyName(), 'desc');
    }

    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $searchable
     * @param  list<string>  $sortable
     * @return LengthAwarePaginator<int, \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model>
     */
    public static function apply(Builder $query, Request $request, array $searchable, array $sortable, string $defaultSort = 'id', string $defaultDir = 'desc'): LengthAwarePaginator
    {
        $perPage = (int) $request->query('per_page', 15);
        $perPage = in_array($perPage, [15, 25, 50, 100], true) ? $perPage : 15;

        /** @var LengthAwarePaginator<int, \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model> $paginator */
        $paginator = self::applyFilters($query, $request, $searchable, $sortable, $defaultSort, $defaultDir)
            ->paginate($perPage)
            ->withQueryString();

        return $paginator;
    }
}
```
Searchable columns must be fully qualified when the query has joins (the task says so when needed).

- [ ] **Step 8: Install Laravel Excel and write `app/Exports/TableExport.php`**

Run: `composer require "maatwebsite/excel:^4.0" --no-interaction 2>&1 | tail -2 && php artisan vendor:publish --provider="Maatwebsite\Excel\ExcelServiceProvider" --tag=config --no-interaction && test -f config/excel.php && echo OK`
Expected: `OK`.

```php
<?php

namespace App\Exports;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\Exportable;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\FromQuery;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\WithCustomCsvSettings;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\WithHeadings;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\WithMapping;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\WithStrictNullComparison;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Excel;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\BinaryFileResponse;

final class TableExport implements FromQuery, WithCustomCsvSettings, WithHeadings, WithMapping, WithStrictNullComparison
{
    use Exportable;

    /** @param array<string, string|Closure> $columns heading => dot path on the model, or Closure(Model): mixed */
    public function __construct(private readonly Builder $builder, private readonly array $columns) {}

    /** @param array<string, string|Closure> $columns */
    public static function fromQuery(Builder $query, string $baseName, array $columns, string $format = 'xlsx'): BinaryFileResponse
    {
        $format = $format === 'csv' ? 'csv' : 'xlsx';
        $fileName = $baseName.'-'.now()->format('Ymd-His').'.'.$format;

        activity('exports')->event('exported')->withProperties([
            'table' => $baseName,
            'format' => $format,
            'filters' => request()->query(),
            'row_count' => (clone $query)->toBase()->getCountForPagination(),
        ])->log("Exportación de {$baseName} ({$format})");

        return (new self($query, $columns))->download($fileName, $format === 'csv' ? Excel::CSV : Excel::XLSX);
    }

    public function query(): Builder
    {
        return $this->builder;
    }

    public function headings(): array
    {
        return array_keys($this->columns);
    }

    /** @param Model $row */
    public function map(mixed $row): array
    {
        return array_map(fn ($accessor) => $accessor instanceof Closure ? $accessor($row) : data_get($row, $accessor), array_values($this->columns));
    }

    public function getCsvSettings(): array
    {
        return ['delimiter' => ',', 'use_bom' => true];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write `app/Support/AuditHistory.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Support;

use App\Models\ActivityLog;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

final class AuditHistory
{
    /** @return list<array{id:int, created_at:string, causer:?string, impersonator:?string, event:?string, description:string, changes:list<array{field:string, old:mixed, new:mixed}>, properties:array<string, mixed>}> */
    public static function for(Model $model, int $limit = 100): array
    {
        return ActivityLog::query()->with(['causer', 'impersonator'])->forSubject($model)->latest('id')->limit($limit)->get()
            ->map(function (ActivityLog $a): array {
                $old = $a->attribute_changes['old'] ?? [];
                $new = $a->attribute_changes['attributes'] ?? [];
                $fields = array_values(array_unique([...array_keys($old), ...array_keys($new)]));

                return [
                    'id' => $a->id,
                    'created_at' => $a->created_at->toIso8601String(),
                    'causer' => $a->causer?->name,
                    'impersonator' => $a->impersonator?->name,
                    'event' => $a->event,
                    'description' => $a->description,
                    'changes' => array_map(fn (string $f) => ['field' => $f, 'old' => $old[$f] ?? null, 'new' => $new[$f] ?? null], $fields),
                    'properties' => $a->properties?->toArray() ?? [],
                ];
            })->all();
    }
}
```
