`app/Services/Settings.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Services;

use App\Models\Setting;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Cache;

class Settings
{
    private const CACHE_KEY = 'settings.all';

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    private function all(): array
    {
        return Cache::rememberForever(self::CACHE_KEY, fn () => Setting::query()->pluck('value', 'key')->map(fn ($v) => $v['v'] ?? null)->all());
    }

    public function get(string $key, mixed $default = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->all()[$key] ?? $default;
    }

    public function set(string $key, mixed $value): void
    {
        Setting::query()->updateOrCreate(['key' => $key], ['value' => ['v' => $value]]);
        Cache::forget(self::CACHE_KEY);
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function many(array $keys): array
    {
        $all = $this->all();

        return array_combine($keys, array_map(fn ($k) => $all[$k] ?? null, $keys));
    }
}
```
(Values are wrapped as `{"v": ...}` so scalars and `null` round-trip through jsonb.) Register in `AppServiceProvider::register()`: `$this->app->singleton(\App\Services\Settings::class);`.

- [ ] **Step 11: Shared CRUD test helper `tests/Support/crud.php`** and require it from `tests/Pest.php` (append `require __DIR__.'/Support/crud.php';` after the helpers require)

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

/**
 * Registers the 10 shared CRUD tests of GC-7 for one resource.
 *
 * @param array{
 *   label: string,                 // test name prefix, e.g. 'brands'
 *   routePrefix: string,           // e.g. 'catalog.brands'
 *   component: string,             // Inertia index component, e.g. 'catalog/brands/index'
 *   model: class-string<\Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model>,
 *   table: string,                 // DB table = activity log name
 *   routeParam: string,            // resource route parameter name, e.g. 'brand'
 *   allowedRole: string,           // role that can do everything on the resource
 *   deniedIndexRole: ?string,      // role that cannot view; null = a user with no roles
 *   deniedStoreRole: string,       // role that can view but not create
 *   payload: Closure(): array,     // valid store payload (may create related rows)
 *   required: list<string>,        // fields reported when posting []
 *   assertColumn: string,          // column asserted after store, taken from the payload
 *   update: array{field: string, value: string},
 *   exportTable: string,           // properties.table written by TableExport
 *   created: string, updated: string, deleted: string   // flash messages
 * } $cfg
 */
function crudResourceTests(array $cfg): void
{
    $L = $cfg['label'];
    $r = $cfg['routePrefix'];
    $model = $cfg['model'];

    test("$L: index is forbidden without permission", function () use ($cfg, $r) {
        if ($cfg['deniedIndexRole'] === null) {
            $this->actingAs(User::factory()->create());
        } else {
            actingAsRole($cfg['deniedIndexRole']);
        }
        $this->get(route("$r.index"))->assertForbidden();
    });

    test("$L: index renders for an allowed role", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $model::factory()->create();
        $this->get(route("$r.index"))->assertOk()->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->component($cfg['component'])->has('rows.data', 1)->has('filters'));
    });

    test("$L: store is forbidden without the create permission", function () use ($cfg, $r) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['deniedStoreRole']);
        $this->post(route("$r.store"), ($cfg['payload'])())->assertForbidden();
    });

    test("$L: store validates required fields", function () use ($cfg, $r) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $this->post(route("$r.store"), [])->assertSessionHasErrors($cfg['required']);
    });

    test("$L: store creates the record and logs it", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        $actor = actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $payload = ($cfg['payload'])();
        $this->post(route("$r.store"), $payload)->assertRedirect(route("$r.index"))->assertSessionHas('success', $cfg['created']);
        $this->assertDatabaseHas($cfg['table'], [$cfg['assertColumn'] => $payload[$cfg['assertColumn']]]);
        $row = $model::query()->where($cfg['assertColumn'], $payload[$cfg['assertColumn']])->firstOrFail();
        expect(Activity::inLog($cfg['table'])->where('event', 'created')->where('subject_id', $row->id)->where('causer_id', $actor->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();
    });

    test("$L: update changes the record and logs old and new values", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        $actor = actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $row = $model::factory()->create();
        $field = $cfg['update']['field'];
        $before = $row->getAttribute($field);
        $payload = array_merge(($cfg['payload'])(), [$field => $cfg['update']['value']]);
        $this->put(route("$r.update", [$cfg['routeParam'] => $row]), $payload)->assertRedirect(route("$r.index"))->assertSessionHas('success', $cfg['updated']);
        expect((string) $row->fresh()->getAttribute($field))->toBe($cfg['update']['value']);
        $activity = Activity::inLog($cfg['table'])->where('event', 'updated')->where('subject_id', $row->id)->latest('id')->firstOrFail();
        expect($activity->causer_id)->toBe($actor->id)
            ->and((string) $activity->attribute_changes['old'][$field])->toBe((string) $before)
            ->and((string) $activity->attribute_changes['attributes'][$field])->toBe($cfg['update']['value']);
    });

    test("$L: destroy deletes the record and logs it", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $row = $model::factory()->create();
        $this->delete(route("$r.destroy", [$cfg['routeParam'] => $row]))->assertRedirect(route("$r.index"))->assertSessionHas('success', $cfg['deleted']);
        $this->assertDatabaseMissing($cfg['table'], ['id' => $row->id]);
        expect(Activity::inLog($cfg['table'])->where('event', 'deleted')->where('subject_id', $row->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();
    });

    test("$L: export downloads an xlsx file", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $model::factory()->create();
        $response = $this->get(route("$r.export", ['format' => 'xlsx']));
        $response->assertOk();
        expect($response->headers->get('content-type'))->toContain('spreadsheetml');
    });

    test("$L: export is logged", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $model::factory()->create();
        $this->get(route("$r.export", ['format' => 'csv']))->assertOk();
        $log = Activity::inLog('exports')->where('event', 'exported')->latest('id')->firstOrFail();
        expect($log->properties['table'])->toBe($cfg['exportTable'])->and($log->properties['format'])->toBe('csv')->and($log->properties['row_count'])->toBe(1);
    });

    test("$L: edit page includes the record history", function () use ($cfg, $r, $model) {
        actingAsRole($cfg['allowedRole']);
        $row = $model::factory()->create();
        $this->get(route("$r.edit", [$cfg['routeParam'] => $row]))->assertOk()->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->has('history'));
    });
}
```

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;

test('search filters with ilike across columns and paginates', function () {
```

```php
<?php

use App\Exports\TableExport;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\AuditHistory;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

test('exports a query to xlsx and csv and logs the export', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    User::factory()->create(['name' => 'Zoé', 'email' => 'zoe@x.test']);
    $columns = ['Nombre' => 'name', 'Correo' => 'email', 'Mayúsculas' => fn (User $u) => strtoupper($u->name)];
    $query = fn () => User::query()->where('email', 'zoe@x.test')->orderBy('id');

    $xlsx = TableExport::fromQuery($query(), 'usuarios', $columns, 'xlsx');
    $csv = TableExport::fromQuery($query(), 'usuarios', $columns, 'csv');

    expect($xlsx->getFile()->getExtension())->toBe('xlsx')
        ->and($csv->getFile()->getExtension())->toBe('csv')
        ->and(file_get_contents($csv->getFile()->getPathname()))->toContain('Nombre,Correo')->toContain('Zoé,zoe@x.test,ZOÉ');

    $log = Activity::inLog('exports')->where('event', 'exported')->latest('id')->firstOrFail();
    expect($log->properties['table'])->toBe('usuarios')->and($log->properties['format'])->toBe('csv')->and($log->properties['row_count'])->toBe(1);
});

test('audit history returns entries for a record', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $u = User::factory()->create(['name' => 'A']);
    $u->update(['name' => 'B']);

    $history = AuditHistory::for($u);

    expect($history)->toHaveCount(2)
        ->and($history[0]['event'])->toBe('updated')
        ->and($history[0]['changes'])->toContain(['field' => 'name', 'old' => 'A', 'new' => 'B'])
        ->and($history[1]['event'])->toBe('created');
});
```

```php
<?php

use App\Models\User;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

test('super admin can impersonate an active user and return', function () {
    $admin = actingAsRole('super-admin');
    $target = User::factory()->create();
    $target->assignRole('sales');

    $this->post(route('admin.users.impersonate', $target))->assertRedirect(route('dashboard'))->assertSessionHas('impersonator_id', $admin->id);
    $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($target);
    expect(Activity::inLog('auth')->where('event', 'impersonation_started')->where('causer_id', $admin->id)->where('subject_id', $target->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();

    $this->get(route('dashboard'))->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->where('auth.impersonator.id', $admin->id));

    $this->delete(route('admin.impersonate.stop'))->assertRedirect(route('admin.users.index'))->assertSessionMissing('impersonator_id');
    $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($admin);
    expect(Activity::inLog('auth')->where('event', 'impersonation_stopped')->where('causer_id', $admin->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();
});

test('owner cannot impersonate', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->post(route('admin.users.impersonate', User::factory()->create()))->assertForbidden();
});

test('cannot impersonate self, an inactive user or another super admin', function () {
    $admin = actingAsRole('super-admin');
    $other = User::factory()->create();
    $other->assignRole('super-admin');

    $this->post(route('admin.users.impersonate', $admin))->assertForbidden();
    $this->post(route('admin.users.impersonate', User::factory()->inactive()->create()))->assertForbidden();
    $this->post(route('admin.users.impersonate', $other))->assertForbidden();
});

test('stop without an active impersonation redirects to dashboard', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->delete(route('admin.impersonate.stop'))->assertRedirect(route('dashboard'));
});
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Admin;

use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class StoreUserRequest extends FormRequest
{
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    {
        $this->merge([
            'email' => strtolower(trim((string) $this->input('email'))),
            // The create form has no switch: a new user is active unless the field is sent.
            'is_active' => $this->has('is_active') ? $this->boolean('is_active') : true,
        ]);
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:120'],
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email', 'max:255', 'unique:users,email'],
            'roles' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'roles.*' => ['string', Rule::in(array_keys(PermissionCatalog::ROLES))],
            'is_active' => ['boolean'],
        ];
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Admin;

use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class UpdateUserRequest extends StoreUserRequest
{
    protected function prepareForValidation(): void
    {
        $this->merge([
            'email' => strtolower(trim((string) $this->input('email'))),
            // Edit form always has the switch (or its hidden twin); absent means unchecked.
            'is_active' => $this->boolean('is_active'),
        ]);
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:120'],
            'email' => ['required', 'string', 'email', 'max:255', Rule::unique('users', 'email')->ignore($this->route('user'))],
            'roles' => ['required', 'array', 'min:1'],
            'roles.*' => ['string', Rule::in(array_keys(PermissionCatalog::ROLES))],
            'is_active' => ['boolean'],
        ];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `app/Actions/Admin/SaveUser.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Admin;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Notifications\UserInvited;
use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class SaveUser
{
    /** @param array{name: string, email: string, roles: list<string>, is_active?: bool} $data */
    public function handle(array $data, User $actor, ?User $user = null): User
    {
        $roles = array_values(array_unique($data['roles']));
        $wantsSuperAdmin = in_array(PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN, $roles, true);
        $isActive = $data['is_active'] ?? true;

        if ($wantsSuperAdmin && ! $actor->isSuperAdmin()) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('Solo un Super Admin puede asignar el rol Super Admin.');
        }
        if ($user !== null) {
            if ($user->isSuperAdmin() && ! $actor->isSuperAdmin()) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('Solo un Super Admin puede editar a otro Super Admin.');
            }
            if ($user->is($actor) && $user->isSuperAdmin() && ! $wantsSuperAdmin) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('No puedes quitarte tu propio rol de Super Admin.');
            }
            if ($user->is($actor) && ! $isActive) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('No puedes desactivar tu propia cuenta.');
            }
            if ($user->is_active !== $isActive && ! $actor->can('admin.users.deactivate')) {
                throw new AuthorizationException('No tienes permiso para activar o desactivar usuarios.');
            }
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $roles, $isActive, $user, $actor): User {
            $isNew = $user === null;
            $activeChanged = $user !== null && $user->is_active !== $isActive;
            $user ??= new User;
            $user->fill(['name' => $data['name'], 'email' => $data['email'], 'is_active' => $isActive])->save();
            $user->syncRoles($roles);
            if ($activeChanged) {
                activity('users')->causedBy($actor)->performedOn($user)->event($isActive ? 'activated' : 'deactivated')
                    ->log($isActive ? "Usuario {$user->name} activado" : "Usuario {$user->name} desactivado");
            }
            if ($isNew) {
                $user->notify(new UserInvited);
            }

            return $user;
        });
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Notifications;

use Illuminate\Bus\Queueable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Messages\MailMessage;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notification;

class UserInvited extends Notification
{
    use Queueable;

    /** @return list<string> */
    public function via(object $notifiable): array
    {
        return ['mail']; // deliberate: the invitee has no session, so no database channel (ARCH §7 exception)
    }

    public function toMail(object $notifiable): MailMessage
    {
        return (new MailMessage)
            ->subject('Invitación a Gomez ERP')
            ->greeting("Hola, {$notifiable->name}")
            ->line('Te han invitado a usar Gomez ERP. Inicia sesión con tu cuenta de Google Workspace de la empresa.')
            ->action('Iniciar sesión', route('login'))
            ->salutation('Gomez ERP');
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Controllers**

`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/UserController.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Actions\Admin\SaveUser;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\StoreUserRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\UpdateUserRequest;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\AuditHistory;
use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use App\Support\TableQuery;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class UserController extends Controller
{
    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.view')]
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $query = User::query()->with('roles');
        if ($role = $request->query('role')) {
            $query->whereHas('roles', fn ($q) => $q->where('name', $role));
        }
        if ($request->query('status') === 'active') {
            $query->where('is_active', true);
        } elseif ($request->query('status') === 'inactive') {
            $query->where('is_active', false);
        }

        $rows = TableQuery::apply($query, $request, ['name', 'email'], ['name', 'email', 'last_login_at', 'is_active'], 'name', 'asc')
            ->through(fn (User $u) => [...$u->only(['id', 'name', 'email', 'avatar_url', 'is_active', 'last_login_at']), 'roles' => $u->getRoleNames()->all()]);

        return Inertia::render('admin/users/index', [
            'rows' => $rows,
            'filters' => $request->only(['search', 'sort', 'dir', 'per_page', 'role', 'status']),
            'roleOptions' => $this->roleOptions(false),
            'canImpersonate' => $request->user()->can('admin.users.impersonate'),
            'authId' => $request->user()->id,
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.create')]
    public function create(Request $request): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/users/form', [
            'roleOptions' => $this->roleOptions(! $request->user()->isSuperAdmin()),
            'authId' => $request->user()->id,
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.create')]
    public function store(StoreUserRequest $request, SaveUser $action): RedirectResponse
    {
        $action->handle($request->validated(), $request->user());

        return redirect()->route('admin.users.index')->with('success', 'Usuario invitado');
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.update')]
    public function edit(Request $request, User $user): Response
    {
        return Inertia::render('admin/users/form', [
            'user' => [...$user->only(['id', 'name', 'email', 'is_active']), 'roles' => $user->getRoleNames()->all()],
            'history' => AuditHistory::for($user),
            'roleOptions' => $this->roleOptions(! $request->user()->isSuperAdmin()),
            'authId' => $request->user()->id,
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.update')]
    public function update(UpdateUserRequest $request, User $user, SaveUser $action): RedirectResponse
    {
        $action->handle($request->validated(), $request->user(), $user);

        return redirect()->route('admin.users.index')->with('success', 'Usuario actualizado');
    }

    /** @return list<array{name: string, label: string}> */
    private function roleOptions(bool $hideSuperAdmin): array
    {
        return collect(PermissionCatalog::ROLES)
            ->reject(fn (string $label, string $name) => $hideSuperAdmin && $name === PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN)
            ->map(fn (string $label, string $name) => ['name' => $name, 'label' => $label])
            ->values()->all();
    }
}
```
`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ImpersonateController.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Auth;

class ImpersonateController extends Controller
{
    #[Middleware('permission:admin.users.impersonate')]
    public function start(Request $request, User $user): RedirectResponse
    {
        $actor = $request->user();
        abort_if($user->is($actor), 403, 'No puedes entrar como tú mismo.');
        abort_if(! $user->is_active, 403, 'El usuario está desactivado.');
        abort_if($user->isSuperAdmin(), 403, 'No se puede entrar como otro Super Admin.');

        activity('auth')->causedBy($actor)->performedOn($user)->event('impersonation_started')->withProperties(['ip' => $request->ip()])->log("Entró como {$user->name}");
        $request->session()->put('impersonator_id', $actor->id);
        Auth::guard('web')->login($user);
        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->route('dashboard')->with('success', "Ahora estás viendo el sistema como {$user->name}");
    }

    public function stop(Request $request): RedirectResponse
    {
        $impersonatorId = $request->session()->get('impersonator_id');
        if (! $impersonatorId) {
            return redirect()->route('dashboard');
        }
        $impersonator = User::query()->findOrFail($impersonatorId);
        $impersonated = $request->user();
        $request->session()->forget('impersonator_id');
        activity('auth')->causedBy($impersonator)->performedOn($impersonated)->event('impersonation_stopped')->log("Salió del modo de suplantación de {$impersonated->name}");
        Auth::guard('web')->login($impersonator);
        $request->session()->regenerate();

        return redirect()->route('admin.users.index')->with('success', 'Has vuelto a tu cuenta');
    }
}
```
(`stop` carries no permission middleware; the session key is the authorization. `session()->regenerate()` keeps session data; never use `invalidate()` here.)

```php
class UpdateRolePermissionsRequest extends FormRequest

    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Action `app/Actions/Admin/SyncRolePermissions.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Admin;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

class SyncRolePermissions
{
    private const TECHNICAL = ['admin.system.manage', 'admin.users.impersonate'];

    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $permissionNames
     * @return array{added: list<string>, removed: list<string>}
     */
    public function handle(Role $role, array $permissionNames, User $actor): array
    {
        if ($role->name === PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('El rol Super Admin no se puede modificar.');
        }
        if (! $actor->isSuperAdmin() && array_intersect(self::TECHNICAL, $permissionNames) !== []) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('Solo un Super Admin puede otorgar permisos técnicos.');
        }

        $before = $role->permissions->pluck('name')->all();
        $role->syncPermissions($permissionNames);
        $after = array_values($permissionNames);
        $diff = ['added' => array_values(array_diff($after, $before)), 'removed' => array_values(array_diff($before, $after))];

        activity('roles')->causedBy($actor)->performedOn($role)->event('permissions_updated')->withProperties($diff)->log("Permisos del rol {$role->name} actualizados");
        app(PermissionRegistrar::class)->forgetCachedPermissions();

        return $diff;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Controller `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/RoleController.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Actions\Admin\SyncRolePermissions;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\UpdateRolePermissionsRequest;
use App\Support\PermissionCatalog;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;

class RoleController extends Controller
{
    #[Middleware('permission:admin.roles.view')]
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $roles = collect(PermissionCatalog::ROLES)->map(function (string $label, string $name): array {
            $permissions = $name === PermissionCatalog::SUPER_ADMIN
                ? PermissionCatalog::names()
                : Role::findByName($name)->permissions->pluck('name')->values()->all();

            return ['name' => $name, 'label' => $label, 'permissions' => $permissions];
        })->values()->all();

        $permissions = collect(PermissionCatalog::permissions())
            ->map(fn (array $def, string $name) => ['name' => $name, 'label' => $def['label'], 'module' => $def['module']])
            ->values()->all();

        return Inertia::render('admin/roles/index', [
            'roles' => $roles,
            'modules' => PermissionCatalog::modules(),
            'permissions' => $permissions,
            'isSuperAdmin' => $request->user()->isSuperAdmin(),
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.roles.update')]
    public function update(UpdateRolePermissionsRequest $request, Role $role, SyncRolePermissions $action): RedirectResponse
    {
        $action->handle($role, $request->validated('permissions'), $request->user());
        $label = PermissionCatalog::ROLES[$role->name] ?? $role->name;

        return redirect()->route('admin.roles.index')->with('success', "Permisos del rol {$label} actualizados");
    }
}
```
(The route parameter `{role:name}` binds `Spatie\Permission\Models\Role` by its `name` column; the seeder guarantees the six roles exist.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `app/Support/Money/Decimal.php`**

```php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Support\Money;

use Brick\Math\BigDecimal;
use Brick\Math\RoundingMode;
use TypeError;

final class Decimal
{
    public const MONEY = 2;

    public const QTY = 4;

    public const RATE = 6;

    /** @param string|int|BigDecimal $value never a float */
    public static function of(mixed $value): BigDecimal
    {
        if (is_float($value)) {
            throw new TypeError('Decimal::of no acepta float');
        }
        if ($value instanceof BigDecimal) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (! is_string($value) && ! is_int($value)) {
            throw new TypeError('Decimal::of acepta string, int o BigDecimal');
        }

        return BigDecimal::of($value);
    }

    public static function scale(string|int|BigDecimal $value, int $scale): string
    {
        return (string) self::of($value)->toScale($scale, RoundingMode::HALF_UP);
    }

    public static function money(string|int|BigDecimal $value): string
    {
        return self::scale($value, self::MONEY);
    }

    public static function qty(string|int|BigDecimal $value): string
    {
        return self::scale($value, self::QTY);
    }

    public static function rate(string|int|BigDecimal $value): string
    {
        return self::scale($value, self::RATE);
    }

    public static function mul(string|BigDecimal $a, string|BigDecimal $b, int $scale): string
    {
        return self::scale(self::of($a)->multipliedBy(self::of($b)), $scale);
    }

    public static function div(string|BigDecimal $a, string|BigDecimal $b, int $scale): string
    {
        return (string) self::of($a)->dividedBy(self::of($b), $scale, RoundingMode::HALF_UP);
    }

    public static function percent(string|BigDecimal $base, string|BigDecimal $pct, int $scale = self::MONEY): string
    {
        return (string) self::of($base)->multipliedBy(self::of($pct))->dividedBy(100, $scale, RoundingMode::HALF_UP);
    }

    /** @param iterable<string|int|BigDecimal> $values */
    public static function sum(iterable $values, int $scale = self::MONEY): string
    {
        $total = BigDecimal::zero();
        foreach ($values as $v) {
            $total = $total->plus(self::of($v));
        }

        return self::scale($total, $scale);
    }

    public static function isZero(string|BigDecimal $value): bool
    {
        return self::of($value)->isZero();
    }

    public static function cmp(string|BigDecimal $a, string|BigDecimal $b): int
    {
        return self::of($a)->compareTo(self::of($b));
    }
}
```

### Task 12: RNC and cédula validation (`FiscalId`, validation rule)

**Sources (verified 2026-10-29):** python-stdnum `stdnum/do/rnc.py` (weights `7,9,8,6,5,4,3,2`, `check = Σ(w·d) mod 11`, digit `= (10 − check) mod 9 + 1`; test vectors `101850043` valid, `101850042` invalid, format `131246796` → `1-31-24679-6`) and `stdnum/do/cedula.py` (11 digits, Luhn over the whole number; vectors `00113918205` valid, `00113918204` invalid, format `22400022111` → `224-0002211-1`): https://github.com/arthurdejong/python-stdnum/blob/master/stdnum/do/rnc.py and `/stdnum/do/cedula.py`. The RNC formula is algebraically the usual "r=0→2, r=1→1, else 11−r". The DGII publishes no algorithm document; stdnum also keeps whitelists of real numbers that fail the checksum — **not implemented** (planner decision, Appendix B): a real number that fails the checksum is saved through the audited override (Tasks 19/20).

**Files:**
- Create: `app/Support/FiscalId.php`, `app/Rules/DominicanTaxId.php`, `tests/Unit/Support/FiscalIdTest.php`, `tests/Feature/Support/DominicanTaxIdRuleTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `FiscalId::normalize(string): string` (digits only), `FiscalId::isValidRnc(string): bool`, `FiscalId::isValidCedula(string): bool`, `FiscalId::rncCheckDigit(string $firstEight): int`, `FiscalId::format(string): string`; rule `new DominicanTaxId('rnc'|'cedula')` with messages `El RNC debe tener 9 dígitos y un dígito verificador válido.` / `La cédula debe tener 11 dígitos y un dígito verificador válido.`

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `app/Support/FiscalId.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Support;

final class FiscalId
{
    private const RNC_WEIGHTS = [7, 9, 8, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2];

    public static function normalize(string $value): string
    {
        return preg_replace('/\D+/', '', $value) ?? '';
    }

    /** True when the input contains nothing but digits, spaces and dashes. */
    private static function onlySeparators(string $value): bool
    {
        return preg_match('/^[\d\s-]*$/', $value) === 1;
    }

    public static function rncCheckDigit(string $firstEight): int
    {
        $sum = 0;
        foreach (self::RNC_WEIGHTS as $i => $w) {
            $sum += $w * (int) $firstEight[$i];
        }

        return (10 - ($sum % 11)) % 9 + 1;
    }

    public static function isValidRnc(string $value): bool
    {
        $n = self::normalize($value);
        if (! self::onlySeparators($value) || strlen($n) !== 9) {
            return false;
        }

        return self::rncCheckDigit(substr($n, 0, 8)) === (int) $n[8];
    }

    public static function isValidCedula(string $value): bool
    {
        $n = self::normalize($value);
        if (! self::onlySeparators($value) || strlen($n) !== 11) {
            return false;
        }

        return self::luhnChecksum($n) === 0;
    }

    /** Luhn: from the rightmost digit, double every second digit, subtract 9 when > 9, sum; valid when sum mod 10 == 0. */
    private static function luhnChecksum(string $digits): int
    {
        $sum = 0;
        $double = false;
        for ($i = strlen($digits) - 1; $i >= 0; $i--) {
            $d = (int) $digits[$i];
            if ($double) {
                $d *= 2;
                if ($d > 9) {
                    $d -= 9;
                }
            }
            $sum += $d;
            $double = ! $double;
        }

        return $sum % 10;
    }

    public static function format(string $value): string
    {
        $n = self::normalize($value);

        return match (strlen($n)) {
            9 => substr($n, 0, 1).'-'.substr($n, 1, 2).'-'.substr($n, 3, 5).'-'.substr($n, 8, 1),
            11 => substr($n, 0, 3).'-'.substr($n, 3, 7).'-'.substr($n, 10, 1),
            default => $n,
        };
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `app/Rules/DominicanTaxId.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Rules;

use App\Support\FiscalId;
use Closure;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Validation\ValidationRule;

class DominicanTaxId implements ValidationRule
{
    public function __construct(private readonly string $type) {}

    public function validate(string $attribute, mixed $value, Closure $fail): void
    {
        $value = (string) $value;
        if ($this->type === 'rnc' && ! FiscalId::isValidRnc($value)) {
            $fail('El RNC debe tener 9 dígitos y un dígito verificador válido.');
        }
        if ($this->type === 'cedula' && ! FiscalId::isValidCedula($value)) {
            $fail('La cédula debe tener 11 dígitos y un dígito verificador válido.');
        }
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Currency extends Model
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\CurrencyFactory> */
    use HasFactory, LogsModelActivity;

    protected $fillable = ['code', 'name', 'symbol', 'decimal_places', 'is_base', 'is_active'];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return ['is_base' => 'boolean', 'is_active' => 'boolean', 'decimal_places' => 'integer'];
    }

    public function scopeActive(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('is_active', true);
    }

    public static function base(): self
    {
        return static::query()->where('is_base', true)->firstOrFail();
    }

    public function rates(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ExchangeRate::class);
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Services\Finance;

use App\Contracts\ExchangeRateSource;
use App\Models\Currency;
use App\Services\Settings;
use App\Support\FetchedRate;
use App\Support\Money\Decimal;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use RuntimeException;

class HttpJsonExchangeRateSource implements ExchangeRateSource
{
    public function __construct(private readonly Settings $settings) {}

    public function name(): string
    {
        return 'http_json';
    }

    public function fetch(Currency $currency, CarbonImmutable $date): ?FetchedRate
    {
        $url = (string) $this->settings->get('exchange_rates.http_json.url', '');
        $path = (string) $this->settings->get('exchange_rates.http_json.json_path', '');
        if ($url === '' || $path === '') {
            throw new RuntimeException('La fuente externa no está configurada (URL y ruta JSON).');
        }
        $url = str_replace(['{currency}', '{date}'], [$currency->code, $date->format('Y-m-d')], $url);
        $json = Http::timeout(15)->acceptJson()->get($url)->throw()->json();
        $value = data_get($json, $path);
        if (! is_numeric($value)) {
            throw new RuntimeException("La respuesta no contiene una tasa numérica en la ruta '{$path}'.");
        }

        return new FetchedRate(Decimal::rate((string) $value), is_array($json) ? $json : []);
    }
}
```
`app/Services/Finance/ExchangeRateService.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Services\Finance;

use App\Contracts\ExchangeRateSource;
use App\Exceptions\MissingExchangeRateException;
use App\Models\Currency;
use App\Models\ExchangeRate;
use App\Services\Settings;
use App\Support\Money\Decimal;
use Carbon\CarbonImmutable;
use Carbon\CarbonInterface;

class ExchangeRateService
{
    public function __construct(private readonly Settings $settings) {}

    public function source(): ExchangeRateSource
    {
        return match ($this->settings->get('exchange_rates.source', 'manual')) {
            'http_json' => app(HttpJsonExchangeRateSource::class),
            default => app(ManualExchangeRateSource::class),
        };
    }

    public function rateFor(Currency|string $currency, CarbonInterface $date): ?ExchangeRate
    {
        $currency = $this->resolve($currency);

        return ExchangeRate::query()->where('currency_id', $currency->id)->whereDate('rate_date', '<=', $date->toDateString())->orderByDesc('rate_date')->first();
    }

    public function convertToBase(string $amount, Currency|string $currency, CarbonInterface $date): string
    {
        $currency = $this->resolve($currency);
        if ($currency->is_base) {
            return Decimal::money($amount);
        }
        $rate = $this->rateFor($currency, $date)
            ?? throw new MissingExchangeRateException("No hay tasa de cambio registrada para {$currency->code} en {$date->format('d/m/Y')} ni fechas anteriores.");

        return Decimal::mul($amount, (string) $rate->rate, Decimal::MONEY);
    }

    /** @return list<ExchangeRate> */
    public function fetchToday(): array
    {
        $today = CarbonImmutable::today();
        $source = $this->source();
        $stored = [];
        foreach (Currency::query()->active()->where('is_base', false)->get() as $currency) {
            if (ExchangeRate::query()->where('currency_id', $currency->id)->whereDate('rate_date', $today->toDateString())->exists()) {
                continue; // never overwrite a rate already registered for today (manual corrections win)
            }
            $fetched = $source->fetch($currency, $today);
            if ($fetched === null) {
                continue;
            }
            $stored[] = ExchangeRate::create(['currency_id' => $currency->id, 'rate_date' => $today->toDateString(), 'rate' => $fetched->rate, 'source' => $source->name(), 'fetched_at' => now(), 'created_by' => null, 'notes' => null]);
        }
        $this->settings->set('exchange_rates.last_fetch_error', null);
        activity('exchange_rates')->event('fetched')->withProperties(['count' => count($stored), 'source' => $source->name()])->log('Tasas de cambio obtenidas: '.count($stored));

        return $stored;
    }

    /** Records a failed automatic fetch (used by the job and by the manual "fetch now" action). */
    public function recordFailure(string $message): void
    {
        $this->settings->set('exchange_rates.last_fetch_error', ['at' => now()->toIso8601String(), 'message' => $message]);
        activity('exchange_rates')->event('fetch_failed')->withProperties(['message' => $message])->log('Falló la obtención automática de tasas');
    }

    private function resolve(Currency|string $currency): Currency
    {
        return $currency instanceof Currency ? $currency : Currency::query()->where('code', $currency)->firstOrFail();
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Job, notification, schedule**

`app/Jobs/FetchDailyExchangeRates.php` (before writing, run Boost `search-docs ["queue job attributes Tries Backoff"]` to confirm the attribute namespaces `Illuminate\Queue\Attributes\Tries` and `Illuminate\Queue\Attributes\Backoff`; if the docs give different namespaces, use those):
```php
<?php

namespace App\Jobs;

use App\Models\User;
use App\Notifications\ExchangeRateFetchFailed;
use App\Services\Finance\ExchangeRateService;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Queue\ShouldQueue;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Queue\Queueable;
use Illuminate\Queue\Attributes\Backoff;
use Illuminate\Queue\Attributes\Tries;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;
use Throwable;

#[Tries(3)]
#[Backoff([60, 300, 900])]
class FetchDailyExchangeRates implements ShouldQueue
{
    use Queueable;

    public function handle(ExchangeRateService $service): void
    {
        $service->fetchToday();
    }

    public function failed(?Throwable $e): void
    {
        $message = $e?->getMessage() ?? 'Error desconocido';
        app(ExchangeRateService::class)->recordFailure($message);
        $users = User::query()->where('is_active', true)->get()->filter(fn (User $u) => $u->can('finance.exchange-rates.manage'));
        Notification::send($users, new ExchangeRateFetchFailed($message));
    }
}
```
`app/Notifications/ExchangeRateFetchFailed.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Notifications;

use Illuminate\Bus\Queueable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Messages\MailMessage;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notification;

class ExchangeRateFetchFailed extends Notification
{
    use Queueable;

    public function __construct(public readonly string $message) {}

    /** @return list<string> */
    public function via(object $notifiable): array
    {
        return ['mail', 'database'];
    }

    public function toMail(object $notifiable): MailMessage
    {
        return (new MailMessage)
            ->subject('No se pudo obtener la tasa de cambio')
            ->line("La obtención automática de la tasa de cambio de hoy falló: {$this->message}")
            ->line('Registra la tasa manualmente en Finanzas → Monedas y tasas.')
            ->action('Registrar tasa', route('finance.exchange-rates.index'));
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function toArray(object $notifiable): array
    {
        return ['message' => $this->message];
    }
}
```
`routes/console.php` — append (imports `App\Jobs\FetchDailyExchangeRates`, `App\Services\Settings`, `Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schedule`):

```php
    ->dailyAt(config('erp.exchange_rates.fetch_time'))
    ->when(fn () => app(Settings::class)->get('exchange_rates.source', 'manual') !== 'manual');
```

```php
Route::middleware(['auth'])->prefix('finance')->name('finance.')->group(function () {
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Requests**

`StoreExchangeRateRequest` (GC-7 request template, `$booleans = []`):
```php
'currency_id' => ['required', Rule::exists('currencies', 'id')->where('is_base', false)],
'rate_date' => ['required', 'date_format:Y-m-d', 'before_or_equal:today'],
'rate' => ['required', 'numeric', 'gt:0', 'regex:/^\d{1,6}(\.\d{1,6})?$/'],
'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
```
`UpdateExchangeRateRequest extends StoreExchangeRateRequest` with `rules()` returning only `'rate' => [...]` and `'notes' => [...]` as above.
`StoreCurrencyRequest` (`$booleans = ['is_active']`):
```php
'code' => ['required', 'string', 'size:3', 'alpha', 'uppercase', 'unique:currencies,code'],
'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
'symbol' => ['required', 'string', 'max:5'],
'decimal_places' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:0,4'],
'is_active' => ['boolean'],
```
`UpdateCurrencyRequest extends StoreCurrencyRequest`: `rules()` omits `code` (immutable; the form disables it) and adds:
```php
public function withValidator(\Illuminate\Validation\Validator $validator): void
{
    $validator->after(function (\Illuminate\Validation\Validator $v): void {
        /** @var \App\Models\Currency $currency */
        $currency = $this->route('currency');
        if ($currency->is_base && $this->boolean('is_active') === false) {
            $v->errors()->add('is_active', 'La moneda base no se puede desactivar.');
        }
    });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Controllers**

`ExchangeRateController`:
- `index` `#[Middleware('permission:finance.exchange-rates.view')]`: props `currencies` = active non-base currencies as `{id, code, name, symbol, latest_rate: {rate_date, rate, source}|null}` (latest by `rate_date`); `rows` = `TableQuery::apply(ExchangeRate::query()->with(['currency:id,code', 'creator:id,name'])` filtered by `currency_id` when present, `$request, [], ['rate_date', 'rate'], 'rate_date', 'desc')->through(fn (ExchangeRate $r) => ['id' => $r->id, 'rate_date' => $r->rate_date->format('Y-m-d'), 'rate' => (string) $r->rate, 'source' => $r->source->value, 'source_label' => $r->source->label(), 'fetched_at' => $r->fetched_at?->toIso8601String(), 'notes' => $r->notes, 'currency' => ['id' => $r->currency->id, 'code' => $r->currency->code], 'creator' => $r->creator ? ['name' => $r->creator->name] : null])`; `filters` (`sort, dir, per_page, currency_id`); `sourceType` (setting, default `manual`); `lastFetchError` (setting); `today` (`now()->toDateString()`); `canManage` (`can('finance.exchange-rates.manage')`).
- `store` `#[Middleware('permission:finance.exchange-rates.manage')]`: `ExchangeRate::updateOrCreate(['currency_id' => $v['currency_id'], 'rate_date' => $v['rate_date']], ['rate' => Decimal::rate($v['rate']), 'source' => 'manual', 'fetched_at' => null, 'created_by' => $request->user()->id, 'notes' => $v['notes'] ?? null])` → redirect index `'Tasa registrada'`.
- `update` `#[Middleware('permission:finance.exchange-rates.manage')]`: `$exchangeRate->update(['rate' => Decimal::rate($v['rate']), 'notes' => $v['notes'] ?? null, 'source' => 'manual', 'created_by' => $request->user()->id])` → `'Tasa actualizada'`.
- `fetch` `#[Middleware('permission:finance.exchange-rates.manage')]`: if `$service->source()->name() === 'manual'` → `back()->with('error', 'La fuente configurada es manual; no hay nada que obtener')`; else `try { $n = count($service->fetchToday()); return back()->with('success', "Tasas obtenidas: {$n}"); } catch (\Throwable $e) { $service->recordFailure($e->getMessage()); return back()->with('error', "No se pudo obtener la tasa: {$e->getMessage()}"); }`.

`CurrencyController` (GC-7 pattern; **all** methods `#[Middleware('permission:finance.currencies.manage')]`; no `destroy`, no `export`; index `TableQuery::apply(Currency::query(), $request, ['code', 'name'], ['code', 'name', 'is_active'], 'code', 'asc')`; success strings `Moneda creada` / `Moneda actualizada`).

- [ ] **Step 9: Frontend**

`resources/js/types/finance.ts`:
```ts
export type Currency = { id: number; code: string; name: string; symbol: string; decimal_places: number; is_base: boolean; is_active: boolean };
export type CurrencyWithLatest = Pick<Currency, 'id' | 'code' | 'name' | 'symbol'> & { latest_rate: { rate_date: string; rate: string; source: string } | null };
export type ExchangeRateRow = {
    id: number; rate_date: string; rate: string; source: 'manual' | 'http_json'; source_label: string; fetched_at: string | null; notes: string | null;
    currency: { id: number; code: string }; creator: { name: string } | null;
};
```
`pages/finance/exchange-rates/index.tsx` (title "Monedas y tasas de cambio"; breadcrumb "Monedas y tasas"); props `{ currencies: CurrencyWithLatest[]; rows: Paginated<ExchangeRateRow>; filters; sourceType: 'manual' | 'http_json'; lastFetchError: { at: string; message: string } | null; today: string; canManage: boolean }`:
- `lastFetchError` → `<Alert variant="destructive">` with text `La última obtención automática falló el {formatDateTime(at)}: {message}. Registra la tasa manualmente.`
- Cards grid, one `Card` per currency: title `{code} — {name}`; body `latest_rate ? \`1 ${code} = RD$ ${formatNumber(rate, 4)} (${formatDate(rate_date)}, ${source})\` : 'Sin tasa registrada'`.
- When `canManage`: `Card` "Registrar tasa" with `<Form {...store.form()}>`: `Select name="currency_id"` ("Moneda"), `Input type="date" name="rate_date" defaultValue={today}` ("Fecha"), `Input name="rate" inputMode="decimal"` ("Tasa (RD$ por 1 unidad)"), `Input name="notes"` ("Notas"), button "Guardar". Text `Fuente configurada: {sourceType === 'manual' ? 'Manual' : 'Servicio externo (HTTP JSON)'} — se cambia en Configuración.` and, when `sourceType === 'http_json'`, `<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.post(fetch.url())}>Obtener ahora desde la fuente externa</Button>`.
- `DataTable`: columns Fecha (`rate_date`, sortable, `formatDate`), Moneda (`currency.code`), Tasa (`rate`, sortable, `formatNumber(rate, 6)`), Fuente (`source_label`), Registrada por (`creator?.name ?? 'Sistema'`), Notas. Toolbar: currency `Select` (placeholder "Todas las monedas"). Row action when `canManage`: button "Corregir" opening a `Dialog` with `<Form {...update.form(row.id)}>` (fields `rate` default `row.rate`, `notes` default `row.notes ?? ''`; button "Guardar").
- Link button "Monedas" → `/finance/currencies` when `can('finance.currencies.manage')`.
`pages/finance/currencies/index.tsx` and `form.tsx` per GC-7 (no export, no delete; title "Monedas", button "Nueva moneda"; form fields: `code` [`maxLength={3}`, `className="uppercase"`, `disabled={!!currency}`], `name`, `symbol`, `decimal_places` [`type="number" min={0} max={4}`], `is_active` `Switch` [`disabled={currency?.is_base}`, hint "La moneda base siempre está activa"] plus `<input type="hidden" name="is_active" value="on" />` when `currency?.is_base`).

`tests/Feature/Finance/ExchangeRateServiceTest.php`:
```php
<?php

use App\Exceptions\MissingExchangeRateException;
use App\Jobs\FetchDailyExchangeRates;
use App\Models\Currency;
use App\Models\ExchangeRate;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Notifications\ExchangeRateFetchFailed;
use App\Services\Finance\ExchangeRateService;
use App\Services\Settings;
use Database\Seeders\CurrencySeeder;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Http;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Notification;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

beforeEach(fn () => (new CurrencySeeder)->run());

function configureHttpSource(string $path): void
{
    $settings = app(Settings::class);
    $settings->set('exchange_rates.source', 'http_json');
    $settings->set('exchange_rates.http_json.url', 'https://rates.test/{currency}/{date}');
    $settings->set('exchange_rates.http_json.json_path', $path);
}

test('seeder creates base DOP and USD', function () {
    expect(Currency::base()->code)->toBe('DOP')->and(Currency::where('code', 'USD')->exists())->toBeTrue();
});

test('rateFor uses the exact date or the most recent earlier one', function () {
    $usd = Currency::where('code', 'USD')->first();
    ExchangeRate::factory()->create(['currency_id' => $usd->id, 'rate_date' => '2026-10-01', 'rate' => '58.000000']);
    ExchangeRate::factory()->create(['currency_id' => $usd->id, 'rate_date' => '2026-10-10', 'rate' => '58.500000']);
    $s = app(ExchangeRateService::class);

    expect($s->rateFor('USD', Carbon::parse('2026-10-10'))->rate)->toBe('58.500000')
        ->and($s->rateFor('USD', Carbon::parse('2026-10-05'))->rate)->toBe('58.000000')
        ->and($s->rateFor('USD', Carbon::parse('2026-09-30')))->toBeNull()
        ->and($s->convertToBase('100', 'USD', Carbon::parse('2026-10-20')))->toBe('5850.00')
        ->and($s->convertToBase('100.004', 'DOP', Carbon::parse('2026-10-20')))->toBe('100.00');
});

test('convertToBase rounds half up and throws when no rate exists', function () {
    $usd = Currency::where('code', 'USD')->first();
    ExchangeRate::factory()->create(['currency_id' => $usd->id, 'rate_date' => '2026-10-01', 'rate' => '58.123456']);
    $s = app(ExchangeRateService::class);

    expect($s->convertToBase('100', $usd, Carbon::parse('2026-10-01')))->toBe('5812.35');
    expect(fn () => $s->convertToBase('1', 'USD', Carbon::parse('2026-01-01')))
        ->toThrow(MissingExchangeRateException::class, 'No hay tasa de cambio registrada para USD en 01/01/2026 ni fechas anteriores.');
});

test('manual source fetches nothing', function () {
    expect(app(ExchangeRateService::class)->fetchToday())->toBe([])->and(ExchangeRate::count())->toBe(0);
});

test('http json source stores today rate from the configured path and logs it', function () {
    configureHttpSource('data.sell');
    Http::fake(['rates.test/USD/'.now()->toDateString() => Http::response(['data' => ['sell' => '58.50']])]);

    $stored = app(ExchangeRateService::class)->fetchToday();

    expect($stored)->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and($stored[0]->rate)->toBe('58.500000')
        ->and($stored[0]->source->value)->toBe('http_json')
        ->and($stored[0]->fetched_at)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and(Activity::inLog('exchange_rates')->where('event', 'fetched')->latest('id')->first()->properties['count'])->toBe(1);
});

test('scheduled fetch does not overwrite an existing manual rate', function () {
    $usd = Currency::where('code', 'USD')->first();
    ExchangeRate::factory()->create(['currency_id' => $usd->id, 'rate_date' => now()->toDateString(), 'rate' => '57.000000', 'source' => 'manual']);
    configureHttpSource('rate');
    Http::fake(['rates.test/*' => Http::response(['rate' => 60])]);

    expect(app(ExchangeRateService::class)->fetchToday())->toBe([])
        ->and(ExchangeRate::where('currency_id', $usd->id)->whereDate('rate_date', now()->toDateString())->first()->rate)->toBe('57.000000');
});

test('http json source with a wrong path throws a spanish error', function () {
    configureHttpSource('nope');
    Http::fake(['rates.test/*' => Http::response(['rate' => '58'])]);

    expect(fn () => app(ExchangeRateService::class)->fetchToday())->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, "La respuesta no contiene una tasa numérica en la ruta 'nope'.");
});

test('job failure notifies managers and records and logs the error', function () {
    Notification::fake();
    $owner = actingAsRole('owner');
    $sales = User::factory()->create();
    $sales->assignRole('sales');

    (new FetchDailyExchangeRates)->failed(new RuntimeException('timeout'));

    Notification::assertSentTo($owner, ExchangeRateFetchFailed::class);
    Notification::assertNotSentTo($sales, ExchangeRateFetchFailed::class);
    expect(app(Settings::class)->get('exchange_rates.last_fetch_error')['message'])->toBe('timeout')
        ->and(Activity::inLog('exchange_rates')->where('event', 'fetch_failed')->exists())->toBeTrue();
});
```

```php
<?php

        ->assertRedirect(route('finance.exchange-rates.index'))->assertSessionHas('success', 'Tasa registrada');
    $this->post(route('finance.exchange-rates.store'), ['currency_id' => $usd->id, 'rate_date' => '2026-10-29', 'rate' => '58.20']);

    $settings->set('exchange_rates.http_json.url', 'https://rates.test/{currency}/{date}');

    Http::fake(['rates.test/*' => Http::response('error', 500)]);

    $this->post(route('finance.exchange-rates.fetch'))->assertRedirect()->assertSessionHas('error');

    expect($settings->get('exchange_rates.last_fetch_error'))->not->toBeNull()
        ->and(Activity::inLog('exchange_rates')->where('event', 'fetch_failed')->exists())->toBeTrue();
});
```

```php
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->post(route('finance.currencies.store'), validCurrencyPayload());
    $eur = Currency::where('code', 'EUR')->firstOrFail();
    $this->put(route('finance.currencies.update', $eur), ['name' => 'Euro europeo', 'symbol' => '€', 'decimal_places' => 2, 'is_active' => 'on']);

    $updated = Activity::inLog('currencies')->where('event', 'updated')->where('subject_id', $eur->id)->latest('id')->firstOrFail();
    expect(Activity::inLog('currencies')->where('event', 'created')->where('subject_id', $eur->id)->exists())->toBeTrue()
        ->and($updated->attribute_changes['old']['name'])->toBe('Euro')
        ->and($updated->attribute_changes['attributes']['name'])->toBe('Euro europeo');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Request `app/Http/Requests/Admin/UpdateSettingsRequest.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Admin;

use App\Rules\DominicanTaxId;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class UpdateSettingsRequest extends FormRequest
{
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        return true;
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'company.name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:120'],
            'company.legal_name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'company.rnc' => ['nullable', 'string', new DominicanTaxId('rnc')],
            'company.address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'company.phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'company.email' => ['nullable', 'email', 'max:255'],
            'logo' => ['nullable', 'image', 'max:2048'],
            'exchange_rates.source' => ['required', Rule::in(['manual', 'http_json'])],
            // Laravel's `url` rule rejects the {currency}/{date} placeholders, hence the regex.
            'exchange_rates.http_json.url' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500', 'required_if:exchange_rates.source,http_json', 'regex:/^https?:\/\/\S+$/i'],
            'exchange_rates.http_json.json_path' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100', 'required_if:exchange_rates.source,http_json'],
        ];
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    public function attributes(): array
    {
        return [
            'company.name' => 'el nombre', 'company.legal_name' => 'la razón social', 'company.rnc' => 'el RNC', 'company.address' => 'la dirección',
            'company.phone' => 'el teléfono', 'company.email' => 'el correo electrónico', 'logo' => 'el logo', 'exchange_rates.source' => 'la fuente',
            'exchange_rates.http_json.url' => 'la URL', 'exchange_rates.http_json.json_path' => 'la ruta JSON',
        ];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Controller `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SettingsController.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Http\Requests\Admin\UpdateSettingsRequest;
use App\Services\Settings;
use App\Support\FiscalId;
use Illuminate\Http\RedirectResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;

class SettingsController extends Controller
{
    private const KEYS = [
        'company.name', 'company.legal_name', 'company.rnc', 'company.address', 'company.phone', 'company.email', 'company.logo_path',
        'exchange_rates.source', 'exchange_rates.http_json.url', 'exchange_rates.http_json.json_path',
    ];

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.settings.view')]
    public function index(Request $request, Settings $settings): Response
    {
        $values = $settings->many(self::KEYS);

        return Inertia::render('admin/settings/index', [
            'values' => $values,
            'canManage' => $request->user()->can('admin.settings.manage'),
            'logoUrl' => $values['company.logo_path'] ? Storage::disk('public')->url($values['company.logo_path']) : null,
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.settings.manage')]
    public function update(UpdateSettingsRequest $request, Settings $settings): RedirectResponse
    {
        $v = $request->validated();
        $flat = [
            'company.name' => $v['company']['name'],
            'company.legal_name' => $v['company']['legal_name'] ?? null,
            'company.rnc' => isset($v['company']['rnc']) && $v['company']['rnc'] !== '' ? FiscalId::normalize($v['company']['rnc']) : null,
            'company.address' => $v['company']['address'] ?? null,
            'company.phone' => $v['company']['phone'] ?? null,
            'company.email' => $v['company']['email'] ?? null,
            'exchange_rates.source' => $v['exchange_rates']['source'],
            'exchange_rates.http_json.url' => $v['exchange_rates']['http_json']['url'] ?? null,
            'exchange_rates.http_json.json_path' => $v['exchange_rates']['http_json']['json_path'] ?? null,
        ];
        if ($request->hasFile('logo')) {
            $flat['company.logo_path'] = $request->file('logo')->store('logos', 'public');
        }
        foreach ($flat as $key => $value) {
            $settings->set($key, $value === '' ? null : $value);
        }
        activity('settings')->causedBy($request->user())->event('updated')->withProperties(['keys' => array_keys($flat)])->log('Configuración actualizada');

        return redirect()->route('admin.settings.index')->with('success', 'Configuración guardada');
    }
}
```

`StoreUnitOfMeasureRequest` rules: `'code' => ['required', 'string', 'max:10', 'alpha_num', 'unique:units_of_measure,code'], 'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'], 'is_active' => ['boolean']`; `prepareForValidation` additionally merges `'code' => strtoupper(trim((string) $this->input('code')))`. `UpdateUnitOfMeasureRequest`: unique ignores `$this->route('unit')`.

```php
<?php

namespace App\Enums;

enum AdditionalTaxCalculationType: string
{
    case PercentOfBase = 'percent_of_base';
    case FixedPerUnit = 'fixed_per_unit';
    case FixedPerLiter = 'fixed_per_liter';
    case FixedPerDegreeLiter = 'fixed_per_degree_liter';

    public function label(): string

            self::FixedPerLiter => 'Monto fijo por litro',
            self::FixedPerDegreeLiter => 'Monto fijo por litro de alcohol puro',
        };
    }
}
```

`app/Services/Catalog/AdditionalTaxCalculator.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Services\Catalog;

use App\Enums\AdditionalTaxCalculationType;
use App\Models\AdditionalTax;
use App\Support\Money\Decimal;
use Brick\Math\RoundingMode;
use InvalidArgumentException;

class AdditionalTaxCalculator
{
    /** ARCH §4: per-line additional tax amount, 2 decimals HALF_UP. */
    public function forLine(AdditionalTax $tax, string $qty, string $taxableBase, ?string $volumeLiters, ?string $alcoholDegree): string
    {
        $type = $tax->calculation_type instanceof AdditionalTaxCalculationType ? $tax->calculation_type : AdditionalTaxCalculationType::from((string) $tax->calculation_type);

        return match ($type) {
            AdditionalTaxCalculationType::PercentOfBase => Decimal::percent($taxableBase, (string) $tax->rate),
            AdditionalTaxCalculationType::FixedPerUnit => Decimal::mul($qty, (string) $tax->amount, Decimal::MONEY),
            AdditionalTaxCalculationType::FixedPerLiter => Decimal::money(
                Decimal::of($qty)->multipliedBy(Decimal::of($this->requireVolume($volumeLiters)))->multipliedBy(Decimal::of((string) $tax->amount))
            ),
            AdditionalTaxCalculationType::FixedPerDegreeLiter => Decimal::money(
                Decimal::of($qty)
                    ->multipliedBy(Decimal::of($this->requireVolume($volumeLiters)))
                    ->multipliedBy(Decimal::of($this->requireDegree($alcoholDegree)))
                    ->dividedBy(100, 10, RoundingMode::HALF_UP)
                    ->multipliedBy(Decimal::of((string) $tax->amount))
            ),
        };
    }

    private function requireVolume(?string $volumeLiters): string
    {
        if ($volumeLiters === null || $volumeLiters === '' || Decimal::isZero($volumeLiters)) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('El producto no tiene volumen en litros');
        }

        return $volumeLiters;
    }

    private function requireDegree(?string $alcoholDegree): string
    {
        if ($alcoholDegree === null || $alcoholDegree === '' || Decimal::isZero($alcoholDegree)) {
            throw new InvalidArgumentException('El producto no tiene grado alcohólico');
        }

        return $alcoholDegree;
    }
}
```

```php
test('store creates product with additional taxes and liter tax requires volume', function () {

    $this->post(route('catalog.products.store'), array_merge($base, ['volume_liters' => '0.75', 'tracks_lots' => 'on']))->assertRedirect(route('catalog.products.index'))->assertSessionHas('success', 'Producto creado');

    Product::factory()->create(['brand_id' => $brand->id]);
    Product::factory()->create();

    actingAsRole('sales');
    $this->get(route('catalog.products.index', ['brand_id' => $brand->id]))->assertOk()->assertInertia(fn ($p) => $p->where('rows.total', 1));
    $this->get(route('catalog.products.create'))->assertForbidden();

    actingAsRole('warehouse');

    $this->post(route('catalog.products.store'), validProductPayload())->assertForbidden();
});
```

```php
enum ItbisRate: string
{

    case Reduced = '16.00';
    case Exempt = '0.00';

    public function label(): string
    {
```

`app/Models/Product.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsToMany;

class Product extends Model
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\ProductFactory> */
    use HasFactory, LogsModelActivity;

    protected $fillable = [
        'sku', 'barcode', 'name', 'brand_id', 'category_id', 'unit_of_measure_id', 'units_per_box', 'weight_kg', 'volume_liters',
        'alcohol_degree', 'itbis_rate', 'tracks_lots', 'tariff_code', 'minimum_stock', 'description', 'is_active',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'units_per_box' => 'integer', 'weight_kg' => 'decimal:4', 'volume_liters' => 'decimal:4', 'alcohol_degree' => 'decimal:2',
            'itbis_rate' => 'decimal:2', 'tracks_lots' => 'boolean', 'minimum_stock' => 'decimal:4', 'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }

    public function scopeActive(Builder $query): Builder
    {
        return $query->where('is_active', true);
    }

    public function brand(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Brand::class);
    }

    public function category(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(ProductCategory::class, 'category_id');
    }

    public function unit(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(UnitOfMeasure::class, 'unit_of_measure_id');
    }

    public function additionalTaxes(): BelongsToMany
    {
        return $this->belongsToMany(AdditionalTax::class, 'additional_tax_product');
    }
}
```

`app/Actions/Catalog/SaveProduct.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use App\Models\Product;
use Illuminate\Support\Arr;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class SaveProduct
{
    /** @param array<string, mixed> $data validated StoreProductRequest/UpdateProductRequest data */
    public function handle(array $data, ?Product $product = null): Product
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $product): Product {
            $product ??= new Product;
            $attributes = Arr::except($data, ['additional_tax_ids']);
            $attributes['minimum_stock'] = $attributes['minimum_stock'] ?? '0';
            $product->fill($attributes)->save();

            $before = $product->additionalTaxes()->pluck('code')->sort()->values()->all();
            $product->additionalTaxes()->sync($data['additional_tax_ids'] ?? []);
            $after = $product->additionalTaxes()->pluck('code')->sort()->values()->all();
            if ($before !== $after) {
                activity('products')->performedOn($product)->event('additional_taxes_synced')->withProperties(['before' => $before, 'after' => $after])->log('Impuestos adicionales actualizados');
            }

            return $product;
        });
    }
}
```

```php
public function withValidator(\Illuminate\Validation\Validator $validator): void
{
    $validator->after(function (\Illuminate\Validation\Validator $v): void {
        $ids = $this->input('additional_tax_ids', []);
        if (! is_array($ids) || $ids === []) {
            return;
        }
        $types = \App\Models\AdditionalTax::query()->whereIn('id', $ids)->pluck('calculation_type')->map(fn ($t) => $t->value)->all();
        $volume = $this->input('volume_liters');
        $degree = $this->input('alcohol_degree');
        if (array_intersect($types, ['fixed_per_liter', 'fixed_per_degree_liter']) !== [] && ($volume === null || $volume === '')) {
            $v->errors()->add('additional_tax_ids', 'Los impuestos por litro requieren el volumen en litros del producto.');
        }
        if (in_array('fixed_per_degree_liter', $types, true) && ($degree === null || $degree === '')) {
            $v->errors()->add('additional_tax_ids', 'Los impuestos por grado alcohólico requieren el grado alcohólico del producto.');
        }
    });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Actions**

`app/Actions/Catalog/SavePriceList.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use App\Models\PriceList;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class SavePriceList
{
    /** @param array{name: string, currency_id: int, is_default?: bool, is_active?: bool} $data */
    public function handle(array $data, ?PriceList $list = null): PriceList
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $list): PriceList {
            $list ??= new PriceList;
            $list->fill($data)->save();
            if ($list->is_default) {
                PriceList::query()->whereKeyNot($list->id)->where('is_default', true)->get()->each->update(['is_default' => false]);
            }

            return $list;
        });
    }
}
```
`app/Actions/Catalog/SavePriceListItems.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use App\Models\PriceList;
use App\Models\PriceListItem;
use App\Support\Money\Decimal;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class SavePriceListItems
{
    /** @param list<array{product_id: int, price: ?string}> $items */
    public function handle(PriceList $list, array $items): int
    {
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($list, $items): int {
            $changed = 0;
            foreach ($items as $item) {
                $existing = PriceListItem::query()->where('price_list_id', $list->id)->where('product_id', $item['product_id'])->first();
                $price = $item['price'] ?? null;
                if ($price === null || $price === '') {
                    if ($existing !== null) {
                        $existing->delete();
                        $changed++;
                    }

                    continue;
                }
                $price = Decimal::qty((string) $price);
                if ($existing === null) {
                    PriceListItem::create(['price_list_id' => $list->id, 'product_id' => $item['product_id'], 'price' => $price]);
                    $changed++;
                } elseif ($existing->price !== $price) {
                    $existing->update(['price' => $price]);
                    $changed++;
                }
            }
            activity('price_lists')->performedOn($list)->event('items_updated')->withProperties(['changed' => $changed])->log("Precios actualizados ({$changed} cambios)");

            return $changed;
        });
    }
}
```

'Sí' : 'No', 'Activa' => fn (PriceList $l) => $l->is_active ? 'Sí' : 'No']`. Extra methods:
```php
#[Middleware('permission:catalog.price-lists.update')]
public function items(PriceList $priceList): Response
{
    $prices = $priceList->items()->pluck('price', 'product_id');
    $products = Product::query()->active()->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'sku', 'name'])
        ->map(fn (Product $p) => ['id' => $p->id, 'sku' => $p->sku, 'name' => $p->name, 'price' => isset($prices[$p->id]) ? (string) $prices[$p->id] : null])->values()->all();

    return Inertia::render('catalog/price-lists/items', ['list' => $priceList->load('currency:id,code'), 'products' => $products]);
}

#[Middleware('permission:catalog.price-lists.update')]
public function updateItems(UpdatePriceListItemsRequest $request, PriceList $priceList, SavePriceListItems $action): RedirectResponse
{
    $action->handle($priceList, $request->validated('items'));

    return redirect()->route('catalog.price-lists.items', $priceList)->with('success', 'Precios guardados');
}

#[Middleware('permission:catalog.price-lists.export')]
public function exportItems(Request $request, PriceList $priceList): BinaryFileResponse
{
    $query = PriceListItem::query()->with('product:id,sku,name')->where('price_list_id', $priceList->id)->orderBy('id');

    return TableExport::fromQuery($query, 'price-list-items', ['SKU' => 'product.sku', 'Producto' => 'product.name', 'Precio' => 'price'], $request->string('format', 'xlsx')->toString());
}
```

```php
function validClientPayload(array $over = []): array
{
    $currency = \App\Models\Currency::query()->where('is_base', true)->firstOr(fn () => \App\Models\Currency::factory()->create(['code' => 'DOP', 'is_base' => true]));

    return array_merge([
        'code' => '', 'business_name' => 'Distribuidora Ejemplo SRL', 'trade_name' => 'Ejemplo', 'tax_id_type' => 'rnc', 'tax_id' => '1-31-24679-6',
        'taxpayer_type' => 'juridica', 'address' => 'Av. Principal 1', 'city' => 'Santo Domingo', 'phone' => '809-555-0000', 'email' => 'ventas@ejemplo.test',
        'salesperson_id' => '', 'credit_days' => 30, 'credit_limit' => '100000', 'price_list_id' => '', 'currency_id' => $currency->id, 'notes' => '',
        'contacts' => [], 'tax_id_override' => '', 'tax_id_override_reason' => '', 'is_active' => 'on',
    ], $over);
}

/** A syntactically valid RNC whose first eight digits are random (starts with 1). */
function fakeRnc(): string
{
    $first = '1'.str_pad((string) random_int(0, 9999999), 7, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);

    return $first.\App\Support\FiscalId::rncCheckDigit($first);
}
```
