- [ ] **Step 4: Enums, models, factories**

`TaxIdType` and `TaxpayerType` enums in `app/Enums` with the cases/labels from Interfaces (same shape as `ExchangeRateSourceType`).
`app/Models/Client.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use App\Enums\TaxIdType;
use App\Enums\TaxpayerType;
use App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity;
use App\Support\FiscalId;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Casts\Attribute;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class Client extends Model
{
    /** @use HasFactory<\Database\Factories\ClientFactory> */
    use HasFactory, LogsModelActivity;

    protected $fillable = [
        'code', 'business_name', 'trade_name', 'tax_id_type', 'tax_id', 'taxpayer_type', 'address', 'city', 'phone', 'email', 'salesperson_id',
        'credit_days', 'credit_limit', 'price_list_id', 'currency_id', 'notes', 'tax_id_validation_overridden', 'tax_id_override_reason',
        'tax_id_override_by', 'tax_id_override_at', 'is_active',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'tax_id_type' => TaxIdType::class, 'taxpayer_type' => TaxpayerType::class, 'credit_days' => 'integer', 'credit_limit' => 'decimal:2',
            'tax_id_validation_overridden' => 'boolean', 'tax_id_override_at' => 'datetime', 'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }

    protected function formattedTaxId(): Attribute
    {
        return Attribute::get(fn () => $this->tax_id ? FiscalId::format($this->tax_id) : null);
    }

    public function scopeVisibleTo(Builder $query, User $user): Builder
    {
        if ($user->can('catalog.clients.view-all')) {
            return $query;
        }

        return $query->where('salesperson_id', $user->salesperson?->id ?? -1);
    }

    public function contacts(): HasMany
    {
        return $this->hasMany(ClientContact::class);
    }

    public function salesperson(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Salesperson::class);
    }

    public function priceList(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(PriceList::class);
    }

    public function currency(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Currency::class);
    }

    public function taxIdOverrideBy(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(User::class, 'tax_id_override_by');
    }
}
```
`ClientContact`: `use HasFactory, LogsModelActivity;` `$fillable = ['client_id', 'name', 'position', 'email', 'phone', 'is_primary']`; cast `is_primary` boolean; `client(): BelongsTo`.
`ClientFactory::definition()`: `['code' => 'CLI-'.fake()->unique()->numerify('#####'), 'business_name' => fake()->company(), 'trade_name' => null, 'tax_id_type' => 'rnc', 'tax_id' => fakeRnc(), 'taxpayer_type' => 'juridica', 'address' => fake()->streetAddress(), 'city' => 'Santo Domingo', 'phone' => null, 'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(), 'salesperson_id' => null, 'credit_days' => 30, 'credit_limit' => '100000.00', 'price_list_id' => null, 'currency_id' => Currency::query()->where('is_base', true)->value('id') ?? Currency::factory()->create(['code' => 'DOP', 'is_base' => true])->id, 'notes' => null, 'is_active' => true]` — `fakeRnc()` is a test helper, so the factory defines its own private method `private function rnc(): string` with the same body (using `FiscalId::rncCheckDigit`) and calls `$this->rnc()`.
`ClientContactFactory::definition()`: `['client_id' => Client::factory(), 'name' => fake()->name(), 'position' => null, 'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(), 'phone' => null, 'is_primary' => false]`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Shared trait and actions**

`app/Http/Requests/Concerns/ValidatesDominicanTaxId.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Concerns;

use App\Rules\DominicanTaxId;
use Illuminate\Auth\Access\AuthorizationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;
use Illuminate\Validation\Validator;

/**
 * Shared RNC/cédula validation for clients and suppliers: checksum by type, uniqueness,
 * the audited override (permission catalog.tax-ids.override-validation + mandatory reason),
 * and, when a country field is given, the rule that a Dominican tax id only applies to local records.
 */
trait ValidatesDominicanTaxId
{
    protected function addTaxIdValidation(Validator $validator, string $table, ?Model $ignore, string $taxIdField = 'tax_id', string $typeField = 'tax_id_type', ?string $countryField = null): void
    {
        $type = (string) $this->input($typeField);
        $taxId = (string) $this->input($taxIdField);
        $override = $this->boolean('tax_id_override');
        $isLocal = $countryField === null || $this->input($countryField) === 'DO';

        if ($override && ! $this->user()->can('catalog.tax-ids.override-validation')) {
            throw new AuthorizationException('No tienes permiso para forzar la identificación fiscal.');
        }

        $validator->sometimes($taxIdField, [Rule::unique($table, $taxIdField)->ignore($ignore)], fn () => $taxId !== '');

        if ($countryField !== null && ! $isLocal) {
            $validator->sometimes($taxIdField, ['prohibited'], fn () => $taxId !== '');
            $validator->after(function (Validator $v) use ($taxIdField, $taxId): void {
                if ($taxId !== '') {
                    $v->errors()->forget($taxIdField);
                    $v->errors()->add($taxIdField, 'El RNC/cédula solo aplica a proveedores locales.');
                }
            });

            return;
        }

        if (in_array($type, ['rnc', 'cedula'], true) && $taxId !== '') {
            $digits = $type === 'rnc' ? 9 : 11;
            $validator->sometimes($taxIdField, $override ? ["digits:{$digits}"] : [new DominicanTaxId($type)], fn () => true);
        }

        $validator->sometimes('tax_id_override_reason', ['required', 'string', 'max:255'], fn () => $override);
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    protected function taxIdMessages(): array
    {
        return ['tax_id_override_reason.required' => 'Indica el motivo para forzar la identificación fiscal.'];
    }
}
```
`app/Actions/Catalog/ApplyTaxIdOverride.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

class ApplyTaxIdOverride
{
    /**
     * Sets (or clears) the override columns on an already-saved client/supplier and writes the audit row.
     *
     * @param  array<string, mixed>  $data  validated request data (keys tax_id_override, tax_id_override_reason)
     */
    public function handle(Model $record, array $data, User $actor, string $logName): void
    {
        $override = (bool) ($data['tax_id_override'] ?? false);
        if ($override) {
            $reason = (string) $data['tax_id_override_reason'];
            $record->forceFill(['tax_id_validation_overridden' => true, 'tax_id_override_reason' => $reason, 'tax_id_override_by' => $actor->id, 'tax_id_override_at' => now()])->save();
            activity($logName)->causedBy($actor)->performedOn($record)->event('tax_id_validation_overridden')
                ->withProperties(['tax_id' => $record->getAttribute('tax_id'), 'reason' => $reason])
                ->log('Identificación fiscal forzada: '.$reason);

            return;
        }
        if ($record->getAttribute('tax_id_validation_overridden')) {
            $record->forceFill(['tax_id_validation_overridden' => false, 'tax_id_override_reason' => null, 'tax_id_override_by' => null, 'tax_id_override_at' => null])->save();
        }
    }
}
```
`app/Actions/Catalog/ReplaceContacts.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany;

class ReplaceContacts
{
    /** Deletes the current contacts through the models (so they are audited) and creates the given ones. */
    public function handle(HasMany $relation, array $contacts): void
    {
        $relation->get()->each->delete();
        foreach ($contacts as $contact) {
            $relation->create([
                'name' => $contact['name'],
                'position' => $contact['position'] ?? null,
                'email' => $contact['email'] ?? null,
                'phone' => $contact['phone'] ?? null,
                'is_primary' => filter_var($contact['is_primary'] ?? false, FILTER_VALIDATE_BOOL),
            ]);
        }
    }
}
```
`app/Actions/Catalog/SaveClient.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Actions\Catalog;

use App\Models\Client;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Support\Arr;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class SaveClient
{
    public function __construct(private readonly ApplyTaxIdOverride $applyOverride, private readonly ReplaceContacts $replaceContacts) {}

    /** @param array<string, mixed> $data validated StoreClientRequest/UpdateClientRequest data */
    public function handle(array $data, User $actor, ?Client $client = null): Client
    {
        if (! $actor->can('catalog.clients.view-all')) {
            $salesperson = $actor->salesperson ?? throw ValidationException::withMessages(['salesperson_id' => 'Tu usuario no está vinculado a un vendedor.']);
            $data['salesperson_id'] = $salesperson->id;
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($data, $actor, $client): Client {
            $client ??= new Client;
            $client->fill(Arr::except($data, ['contacts', 'tax_id_override', 'tax_id_override_reason']))->save();
            if (blank($client->code)) {
                $client->code = 'CLI-'.str_pad((string) $client->id, 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
                $client->save();
            }
            $this->applyOverride->handle($client, $data, $actor, 'clients');
            $this->replaceContacts->handle($client->contacts(), $data['contacts'] ?? []);

            return $client;
        });
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Policy `app/Policies/ClientPolicy.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\Client;
use App\Models\User;

class ClientPolicy
{
    public function view(User $user, Client $client): bool
    {
        return $this->owns($user, $client);
    }

    public function update(User $user, Client $client): bool
    {
        return $this->owns($user, $client);
    }

    public function delete(User $user, Client $client): bool
    {
        return $this->owns($user, $client);
    }

    private function owns(User $user, Client $client): bool
    {
        return $user->can('catalog.clients.view-all') || ($user->salesperson !== null && $user->salesperson->id === $client->salesperson_id);
    }
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: migrations `2026_10_29_000051_create_suppliers_table.php`, `2026_10_29_000052_create_supplier_contacts_table.php`; `app/Support/Countries.php`; models `Supplier`, `SupplierContact`; factories; `app/Actions/Catalog/SaveSupplier.php`; controller `Catalog\SupplierController`; requests `{Store,Update}SupplierRequest`; pages `catalog/suppliers/{index,form}.tsx`; `tests/Feature/Catalog/SupplierTest.php`
- Modify: `routes/catalog.php`, `resources/js/types/catalog.ts`, `tests/Support/helpers.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ValidatesDominicanTaxId`, `ApplyTaxIdOverride`, `ReplaceContacts` (Task 19), `Currency`.
- Produces: `Countries::OPTIONS: array<string,string>` = `['DO' => 'República Dominicana', 'US' => 'Estados Unidos', 'CN' => 'China', 'ES' => 'España', 'MX' => 'México', 'PA' => 'Panamá', 'CO' => 'Colombia', 'BR' => 'Brasil', 'IT' => 'Italia', 'DE' => 'Alemania', 'FR' => 'Francia', 'NL' => 'Países Bajos', 'GB' => 'Reino Unido', 'IN' => 'India', 'TR' => 'Turquía', 'CA' => 'Canadá']`; `Supplier(code, name, country_code, tax_id, foreign_tax_id, currency_id, credit_days, address, phone, email, notes, override columns, is_active; contacts(), currency(); accessor is_local = country_code === 'DO')`; `SupplierContact`; `SaveSupplier::handle(array $data, User $actor, ?Supplier $supplier = null): Supplier` (code auto `PRV-` + 5-digit id; override and contacts via the shared actions); routes `catalog.suppliers.*`; helper `validSupplierPayload`.

Rule (coordinator ruling): Dominican RNC/cédula (`tax_id`) only for `country_code = 'DO'`; a foreign supplier stores its identifier in `foreign_tax_id` (free text, max 50). A `tax_id` given for a non-DO supplier is rejected with `El RNC/cédula solo aplica a proveedores locales.`

```php
});

test('contacts are replaced on update with audit rows', function () {
    actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->post(route('catalog.suppliers.store'), validSupplierPayload(['contacts' => [['name' => 'Ana', 'position' => '', 'email' => '', 'phone' => '', 'is_primary' => '1']]]));
    $s = Supplier::latest('id')->firstOrFail();

    $this->put(route('catalog.suppliers.update', $s), validSupplierPayload(['contacts' => [['name' => 'Beto', 'position' => '', 'email' => '', 'phone' => '', 'is_primary' => '0']]]))->assertRedirect(route('catalog.suppliers.index'));
    expect($s->fresh()->contacts->pluck('name')->all())->toBe(['Beto'])
        ->and(Activity::inLog('supplier_contacts')->where('event', 'deleted')->where('subject_id', $firstId)->exists())->toBeTrue();
});

test('owner can force an invalid rnc with a reason and accounting cannot', function () {
    $owner = actingAsRole('owner');
    $this->post(route('catalog.suppliers.store'), validSupplierPayload(['country_code' => 'DO', 'foreign_tax_id' => '', 'tax_id' => '101850042', 'tax_id_override' => 'on', 'tax_id_override_reason' => 'Verificado con el proveedor']))->assertRedirect(route('catalog.suppliers.index'));
    $s = Supplier::where('tax_id', '101850042')->firstOrFail();

        ->and(Activity::inLog('suppliers')->where('event', 'tax_id_validation_overridden')->where('subject_id', $s->id)->exists())->toBeTrue();

    actingAsRole('accounting');
    $this->post(route('catalog.suppliers.store'), validSupplierPayload(['name' => 'Otro', 'country_code' => 'DO', 'foreign_tax_id' => '', 'tax_id' => '131246795', 'tax_id_override' => 'on', 'tax_id_override_reason' => 'x']))->assertForbidden();
});
```

```php
public function messages(): array
{

public function withValidator(Validator $validator): void
{
    // Suppliers have no explicit type: 9 digits = RNC, 11 digits = cédula (derived here for the shared trait).

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Controller `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ActivityLogController.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Admin;

use App\Exports\TableExport;
use App\Http\Controllers\Controller;
use App\Models\ActivityLog;
use App\Models\User;
use App\Support\TableQuery;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Routing\Attributes\Controllers\Middleware;
use Inertia\Inertia;
use Inertia\Response;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\BinaryFileResponse;

class ActivityLogController extends Controller
{
    #[Middleware('permission:admin.activity-log.view')]
    public function index(Request $request): Response
    {
        $rows = TableQuery::apply($this->filtered($request), $request, [], ['created_at'], 'created_at', 'desc')
            ->through(fn (ActivityLog $a) => $this->row($a));

        return Inertia::render('admin/activity-log/index', [
            'rows' => $rows,
            'filters' => $request->only(['search', 'sort', 'dir', 'per_page', 'causer_id', 'log_name', 'subject_type', 'subject_id', 'event', 'date_from', 'date_to']),
            'users' => User::query()->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name']),
            'logNames' => ActivityLog::query()->whereNotNull('log_name')->distinct()->orderBy('log_name')->pluck('log_name'),
            'events' => ActivityLog::query()->whereNotNull('event')->distinct()->orderBy('event')->pluck('event'),
        ]);
    }

    #[Middleware('permission:admin.activity-log.view')]
    public function export(Request $request): BinaryFileResponse
    {
        $query = TableQuery::applyFilters($this->filtered($request), $request, [], ['created_at'], 'created_at', 'desc');

        return TableExport::fromQuery($query, 'activity-log', [
            'Fecha' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'Usuario' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->causer?->name ?? 'Sistema',
            'Suplantado por' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->impersonator?->name,
            'Registro' => 'log_name',
            'Evento' => 'event',
            'Descripción' => 'description',
            'Objeto' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->subject_type ? class_basename($a->subject_type).' #'.$a->subject_id : null,
            'Cambios' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->attribute_changes ? json_encode($a->attribute_changes, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) : null,
            'Propiedades' => fn (ActivityLog $a) => $a->properties?->isNotEmpty() ? json_encode($a->properties, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE) : null,
            'IP' => 'ip',
            'Request' => 'request_id',
        ], $request->string('format', 'xlsx')->toString());
    }

    private function filtered(Request $request): Builder
    {
        $query = ActivityLog::query()->with(['causer:id,name', 'impersonator:id,name']);
        if ($id = $request->query('causer_id')) {
            $query->where('causer_id', $id);
        }
        if ($log = $request->query('log_name')) {
            $query->where('log_name', $log);
        }
        if ($event = $request->query('event')) {
            $query->where('event', $event);
        }
        if ($type = $request->query('subject_type')) {
            $query->where('subject_type', 'ILIKE', '%\\'.ltrim((string) $type, '\\'));
        }
        if ($subjectId = $request->query('subject_id')) {
            $query->where('subject_id', $subjectId);
        }
        if ($from = $request->query('date_from')) {
            $query->whereDate('created_at', '>=', $from);
        }
        if ($to = $request->query('date_to')) {
            $query->whereDate('created_at', '<=', $to);
        }
        if ($search = trim((string) $request->query('search', ''))) {
            $query->where('description', 'ILIKE', '%'.$search.'%');
        }

        return $query;
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    private function row(ActivityLog $a): array
    {
        $old = $a->attribute_changes['old'] ?? [];
        $new = $a->attribute_changes['attributes'] ?? [];
        $fields = array_values(array_unique([...array_keys($old), ...array_keys($new)]));

        return [
            'id' => $a->id,
            'created_at' => $a->created_at->toIso8601String(),
            'causer' => $a->causer ? ['id' => $a->causer->id, 'name' => $a->causer->name] : null,
            'impersonator' => $a->impersonator ? ['id' => $a->impersonator->id, 'name' => $a->impersonator->name] : null,
            'ip' => $a->ip,
            'request_id' => $a->request_id,
            'log_name' => $a->log_name,
            'event' => $a->event,
            'description' => $a->description,
            'subject' => $a->subject_type ? class_basename($a->subject_type).' #'.$a->subject_id : null,
            'changes' => array_map(fn (string $f) => ['field' => $f, 'old' => $old[$f] ?? null, 'new' => $new[$f] ?? null], $fields),
            'properties' => $a->properties?->toArray() ?? [],
        ];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Page `admin/activity-log/index.tsx`** (title "Bitácora de actividad", breadcrumb "Bitácora"). Add to `types/admin.ts`: `export type ActivityRow = { id: number; created_at: string; causer: { id: number; name: string } | null; impersonator: { id: number; name: string } | null; ip: string | null; request_id: string | null; log_name: string | null; event: string | null; description: string; subject: string | null; changes: { field: string; old: unknown; new: unknown }[]; properties: Record<string, unknown> };`. - [ ] **Step 1: Test helper `tests/Support/spreadsheets.php`** and append `require __DIR__.'/Support/spreadsheets.php';` to `tests/Pest.php` after the crud require

```php
<?php

use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use PhpOffice\PhpSpreadsheet\Spreadsheet;
use PhpOffice\PhpSpreadsheet\Writer\Csv;
use PhpOffice\PhpSpreadsheet\Writer\Xlsx;

function makeSpreadsheet(array $rows, string $ext = 'xlsx'): UploadedFile
{
    $path = sys_get_temp_dir().'/'.uniqid('imp_', true).'.'.$ext;
    $spreadsheet = new Spreadsheet;
    $spreadsheet->getActiveSheet()->fromArray($rows, null, 'A1');
    $writer = $ext === 'csv' ? new Csv($spreadsheet) : new Xlsx($spreadsheet);
    $writer->save($path);

    return new UploadedFile($path, 'archivo.'.$ext, null, null, true);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `app/Imports/BaseImporter.php`, registry, template**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Imports;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;

abstract class BaseImporter
{
    abstract public static function type(): string;

    abstract public function label(): string;

    abstract public function permission(): string;

    /** @return list<array{key: string, label: string, required: bool, example: string}> */
    abstract public function columns(): array;

    /** Maps user-facing values (labels, "Sí/No", codes) to the values the rules expect. Default: identity. */
    public function normalize(array $row, array $meta): array
    {
        return $row;
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> Laravel rules keyed by column key */
    abstract public function rules(array $row, array $meta): array;

    /** @return array<string, string> */
    public function messages(): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /** Value used for in-file duplicate detection and create/update resolution; null = always create. */
    abstract public function naturalKey(array $row, array $meta): ?string;

    abstract public function findExisting(array $row, array $meta): ?Model;

    abstract public function persist(array $row, ?Model $existing, array $meta): Model;

    /** Extra rules for the upload form (e.g. price_list_id). */
    public function metaRules(): array
    {
        return [];
    }

    /** @return array<string, string> label slug => key */
    public function labelMap(): array
    {
        $map = [];
        foreach ($this->columns() as $column) {
            $map[\Illuminate\Support\Str::slug($column['label'], '_')] = $column['key'];
        }

        return $map;
    }
}
```
`app/Imports/ImporterRegistry.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Imports;

use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\NotFoundHttpException;

final class ImporterRegistry
{
    /** @var list<class-string<BaseImporter>> */
    private const IMPORTERS = [ClientsImporter::class];

    /** @return array<string, BaseImporter> */
    public static function all(): array
    {
        $all = [];
        foreach (self::IMPORTERS as $class) {
            $all[$class::type()] = app($class);
        }

        return $all;
    }

    public static function get(string $type): BaseImporter
    {
        return self::all()[$type] ?? throw new NotFoundHttpException("Importador desconocido: {$type}");
    }
}
```

`app/Imports/TemplateExport.php`:
```php
<?php

namespace App\Imports;

use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\Exportable;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\FromArray;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Concerns\WithHeadings;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Excel;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\BinaryFileResponse;

final class TemplateExport implements FromArray, WithHeadings
{
    use Exportable;

    public function __construct(private readonly BaseImporter $importer) {}

    public static function for(BaseImporter $importer): BinaryFileResponse
    {
        activity('imports')->event('template_downloaded')->withProperties(['type' => $importer::type()])->log('Plantilla descargada: '.$importer->label());

        return (new self($importer))->download('plantilla-'.$importer::type().'.xlsx', Excel::XLSX);
    }

    public function headings(): array
    {
        return array_map(fn (array $c) => $c['label'], $this->importer->columns());
    }

    public function array(): array
    {
        return [array_map(fn (array $c) => $c['example'], $this->importer->columns())];
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `app/Services/DataImport/ImportService.php`**

```php
<?php

namespace App\Services\DataImport;

use App\Enums\ImportBatchStatus;
use App\Imports\BaseImporter;
use App\Models\ImportBatch;
use App\Models\ImportBatchRow;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Validator;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Maatwebsite\Excel\Facades\Excel;
use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\UnprocessableEntityHttpException;

class ImportService
{
    public const MAX_ROWS = 5000;

    public function preview(BaseImporter $importer, UploadedFile $file, User $user, array $meta = []): ImportBatch
    {
        $originalName = $file->getClientOriginalName();
        $path = $file->store('imports/'.now()->format('Y/m'), 'local');
        $sheet = Excel::toCollection(null, $file)->first();
        if ($sheet === null || $sheet->isEmpty()) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['file' => 'El archivo está vacío.']);
        }

        $labelMap = $importer->labelMap();
        $header = [];
        foreach ($sheet->first() as $index => $cell) {
            $key = $labelMap[Str::slug((string) $cell, '_')] ?? null;
            if ($key !== null) {
                $header[$index] = $key;
            }
        }
        $missing = collect($importer->columns())->filter(fn (array $c) => $c['required'] && ! in_array($c['key'], $header, true))->pluck('label');
        if ($missing->isNotEmpty()) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['file' => 'Faltan columnas obligatorias: '.$missing->implode(', ')]);
        }

        $dataRows = $sheet->slice(1)->filter(fn ($row) => collect($row)->contains(fn ($v) => $v !== null && trim((string) $v) !== ''));
        if ($dataRows->isEmpty()) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['file' => 'El archivo está vacío.']);
        }
        if ($dataRows->count() > self::MAX_ROWS) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['file' => 'El archivo supera el máximo de '.self::MAX_ROWS.' filas.']);
        }

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($importer, $user, $meta, $path, $originalName, $header, $dataRows): ImportBatch {
            $batch = ImportBatch::create([
                'type' => $importer::type(), 'file_path' => $path, 'original_name' => $originalName, 'status' => ImportBatchStatus::Previewed,
                'meta' => $meta ?: null, 'user_id' => $user->id,
            ]);
            $seen = [];
            $valid = 0;
            $invalid = 0;
            $buffer = [];
            foreach ($dataRows as $index => $row) {
                $rowNumber = $index + 1; // spreadsheet row (header is row 1)
                $data = [];
                foreach ($header as $col => $key) {
                    $cell = $row[$col] ?? null;
                    $data[$key] = ($cell === null || trim((string) $cell) === '') ? null : trim((string) $cell);
                }
                $data = $importer->normalize($data, $meta);
                $validator = Validator::make($data, $importer->rules($data, $meta), $importer->messages());
                $errors = [];
                foreach ($validator->errors()->toArray() as $field => $messages) {
                    $errors[$field] = $messages[0];
                }
                $key = $importer->naturalKey($data, $meta);
                if ($key !== null && isset($seen[$key])) {
                    $errors['_row'] = 'Valor duplicado en el archivo (fila '.$seen[$key].')';
                } elseif ($key !== null) {
                    $seen[$key] = $rowNumber;
                }
                $isValid = $errors === [];
                $isValid ? $valid++ : $invalid++;
                $buffer[] = [
                    'import_batch_id' => $batch->id, 'row_number' => $rowNumber, 'data' => json_encode($data, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE),
                    'errors' => $errors === [] ? null : json_encode($errors, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE), 'is_valid' => $isValid,
                    'action' => $isValid ? ($importer->findExisting($data, $meta) ? 'update' : 'create') : null,
                ];
                if (count($buffer) >= 500) {
                    ImportBatchRow::insert($buffer);
                    $buffer = [];
                }
            }
            if ($buffer !== []) {
                ImportBatchRow::insert($buffer);
            }
            $batch->update(['total_rows' => $valid + $invalid, 'valid_rows' => $valid, 'invalid_rows' => $invalid]);
            activity('imports')->causedBy($user)->performedOn($batch)->event('previewed')
                ->withProperties(['file' => $originalName, 'total_rows' => $valid + $invalid, 'valid_rows' => $valid, 'invalid_rows' => $invalid])
                ->log("Vista previa de importación de {$importer->label()}");

            return $batch;
        });
    }

    public function confirm(ImportBatch $batch): ImportBatch
    {
        if ($batch->status !== ImportBatchStatus::Previewed) {
            throw new UnprocessableEntityHttpException('La importación ya fue procesada.');
        }
        $importer = \App\Imports\ImporterRegistry::get($batch->type);
        $meta = $batch->meta ?? [];

        return DB::transaction(function () use ($batch, $importer, $meta): ImportBatch {
            $created = 0;
            $updated = 0;
            $batch->rows()->where('is_valid', true)->orderBy('row_number')->chunkById(200, function ($rows) use ($importer, $meta, &$created, &$updated): void {
                foreach ($rows as $row) {
                    $existing = $importer->findExisting($row->data, $meta);
                    $importer->persist($row->data, $existing, $meta);
                    $existing ? $updated++ : $created++;
                }
            });
            $batch->update(['status' => ImportBatchStatus::Confirmed, 'confirmed_at' => now(), 'created_count' => $created, 'updated_count' => $updated]);
            activity('imports')->performedOn($batch)->event('confirmed')->withProperties(['created' => $created, 'updated' => $updated])->log("Importación confirmada: {$created} creados, {$updated} actualizados");

            return $batch;
        });
    }

    public function cancel(ImportBatch $batch): ImportBatch
    {
        if ($batch->status !== ImportBatchStatus::Previewed) {
            throw new UnprocessableEntityHttpException('La importación ya fue procesada.');
        }
        $batch->update(['status' => ImportBatchStatus::Cancelled]);
        activity('imports')->performedOn($batch)->event('cancelled')->log('Importación cancelada');

        return $batch;
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Imports;

use App\Enums\TaxIdType;
use App\Enums\TaxpayerType;

use Illuminate\Validation\Rule;

class ClientsImporter extends BaseImporter
{
    public static function type(): string
    {
        return 'clients';
    }

    public function label(): string
    {
        return 'Clientes';
    }

    public function permission(): string
    {
        return 'catalog.clients.import';
    }

    public function columns(): array
    {
        return [
            ['key' => 'code', 'label' => 'Código', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'CLI-00001'],
            ['key' => 'business_name', 'label' => 'Razón social', 'required' => true, 'example' => 'Distribuidora Ejemplo SRL'],
            ['key' => 'trade_name', 'label' => 'Nombre comercial', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'Ejemplo'],
            ['key' => 'tax_id_type', 'label' => 'Tipo ID', 'required' => true, 'example' => 'RNC'],
            ['key' => 'tax_id', 'label' => 'RNC/Cédula', 'required' => false, 'example' => '1-01-85004-3'],
            ['key' => 'taxpayer_type', 'label' => 'Tipo contribuyente', 'required' => true, 'example' => 'Persona jurídica'],
            ['key' => 'address', 'label' => 'Dirección', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'Av. Principal 1'],
            ['key' => 'city', 'label' => 'Ciudad', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'Santo Domingo'],
            ['key' => 'phone', 'label' => 'Teléfono', 'required' => false, 'example' => '809-555-0000'],
            ['key' => 'email', 'label' => 'Correo', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'ventas@ejemplo.test'],
            ['key' => 'salesperson_code', 'label' => 'Vendedor (código)', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'VEN-01'],
            ['key' => 'credit_days', 'label' => 'Días de crédito', 'required' => false, 'example' => '30'],
            ['key' => 'credit_limit', 'label' => 'Límite de crédito', 'required' => false, 'example' => '100000'],
            ['key' => 'price_list_name', 'label' => 'Lista de precios', 'required' => false, 'example' => 'Distribuidores'],
            ['key' => 'currency_code', 'label' => 'Moneda', 'required' => true, 'example' => 'DOP'],
        ];
    }

    public function normalize(array $row, array $meta): array
    {
        $typeMap = ['rnc' => 'rnc', 'cedula' => 'cedula', 'ninguno' => 'none', 'none' => 'none'];
        $row['tax_id_type'] = $typeMap[Str::slug((string) ($row['tax_id_type'] ?? ''))] ?? $row['tax_id_type'];
        $taxpayerMap = [];
        foreach (TaxpayerType::cases() as $case) {
            $taxpayerMap[Str::slug($case->label())] = $case->value;
            $taxpayerMap[Str::slug($case->value)] = $case->value;
        }
        $row['taxpayer_type'] = $taxpayerMap[Str::slug((string) ($row['taxpayer_type'] ?? ''))] ?? $row['taxpayer_type'];
        $row['tax_id'] = $row['tax_id_type'] === 'none' || empty($row['tax_id']) ? null : FiscalId::normalize((string) $row['tax_id']);
        $row['currency_code'] = isset($row['currency_code']) ? strtoupper($row['currency_code']) : null;
        $row['email'] = isset($row['email']) ? strtolower($row['email']) : null;
        $row['credit_days'] = $row['credit_days'] ?? '0';
        $row['credit_limit'] = $row['credit_limit'] ?? '0';

        return $row;
    }

    public function rules(array $row, array $meta): array
    {
        $taxIdRules = ['nullable', 'string'];
        if (in_array($row['tax_id_type'] ?? null, ['rnc', 'cedula'], true)) {
            $taxIdRules = ['required', 'string', new DominicanTaxId($row['tax_id_type'])];
        }

        return [
            'code' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:20', 'alpha_dash'],
            'business_name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'trade_name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'tax_id_type' => ['required', Rule::enum(TaxIdType::class)],
            'tax_id' => $taxIdRules,
            'taxpayer_type' => ['required', Rule::enum(TaxpayerType::class)],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'city' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'email' => ['nullable', 'email', 'max:255'],
            'salesperson_code' => ['nullable', 'string', 'exists:salespeople,code'],
            'credit_days' => ['required', 'integer', 'between:0,365'],
            'credit_limit' => ['required', 'numeric', 'min:0'],
            'price_list_name' => ['nullable', 'string', 'exists:price_lists,name'],
            'currency_code' => ['required', 'string', 'exists:currencies,code'],
        ];
    }

    public function naturalKey(array $row, array $meta): ?string
    {
        return $row['tax_id'] ?: ($row['code'] ?: null);
    }

    public function findExisting(array $row, array $meta): ?Model
    {
        if (! empty($row['tax_id'])) {
            return Client::query()->where('tax_id', $row['tax_id'])->first();
        }
        if (! empty($row['code'])) {
            return Client::query()->where('code', $row['code'])->first();
        }

        return null;
    }

    public function persist(array $row, ?Model $existing, array $meta): Model
    {
        /** @var Client $client */
        $client = $existing ?? new Client;
        $client->fill([
            'business_name' => $row['business_name'],
            'trade_name' => $row['trade_name'],
            'tax_id_type' => $row['tax_id_type'],
            'tax_id' => $row['tax_id'],
            'taxpayer_type' => $row['taxpayer_type'],
            'address' => $row['address'],
            'city' => $row['city'],
            'phone' => $row['phone'],
            'email' => $row['email'],
            'salesperson_id' => $row['salesperson_code'] ? Salesperson::query()->where('code', $row['salesperson_code'])->value('id') : $client->salesperson_id,
            'credit_days' => (int) $row['credit_days'],
            'credit_limit' => Decimal::money((string) $row['credit_limit']),
            'price_list_id' => $row['price_list_name'] ? PriceList::query()->where('name', $row['price_list_name'])->value('id') : $client->price_list_id,
            'currency_id' => Currency::query()->where('code', $row['currency_code'])->value('id'),
        ]);
        if (! empty($row['code'])) {
            $client->code = $row['code'];
        } elseif (blank($client->code)) {
            $client->code = 'TMP';
        }
        $client->save();
        if ($client->code === 'TMP') {
            $client->code = 'CLI-'.str_pad((string) $client->id, 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
            $client->save();
        }

        return $client;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Request, routes, controller**

`UploadImportRequest`: `rules()` returns `['file' => ['required', 'file', 'mimes:xlsx,xls,csv', 'max:10240']] + ImporterRegistry::get($this->route('type'))->metaRules()`.
Routes (admin group, imports `ImportController`):
```php
Route::middleware('permission:catalog.clients.import|catalog.suppliers.import|catalog.products.import|catalog.price-lists.import')->group(function () {
    Route::get('imports', [ImportController::class, 'index'])->name('imports.index');
    Route::get('imports/{type}/template', [ImportController::class, 'template'])->name('imports.template');
    Route::post('imports/{type}', [ImportController::class, 'preview'])->name('imports.preview');
    Route::get('imports/batches/{batch}', [ImportController::class, 'show'])->name('imports.show');
    Route::post('imports/batches/{batch}/confirm', [ImportController::class, 'confirm'])->name('imports.confirm');
    Route::delete('imports/batches/{batch}', [ImportController::class, 'cancel'])->name('imports.cancel');
});
```
`ImportController` (`$batch` binds `ImportBatch`): every importer-specific method starts with `$importer = ImporterRegistry::get($type)` (or `$batch->type`) and `abort_unless($request->user()->can($importer->permission()), 403);`
- `index`: `importers` = usable importers as `['type', 'label', 'columns' => [['label','required']], 'templateUrl' => route('admin.imports.template', $type), 'meta' => $this->metaOptions($importer)]` where `metaOptions` returns `[]` (Task 25 adds price lists); `batches` = last 20 `ImportBatch` of usable types with `user:id,name`, mapped to `['id','type','label' => importer label,'original_name','status' => value,'status_label','total_rows','valid_rows','invalid_rows','created_count','updated_count','created_at','user' => ['name']]`.
- `template` → `TemplateExport::for($importer)`.
- `preview(UploadImportRequest $request, string $type, ImportService $service)`: `$batch = $service->preview($importer, $request->file('file'), $request->user(), $request->safe()->except('file'))` → `redirect()->route('admin.imports.show', $batch)->with('success', 'Vista previa generada')`.
- `show`: `batch` (as in index), `rows` = `$batch->rows()->orderBy('is_valid')->orderBy('row_number')->paginate(50)->withQueryString()`, `columns` = `[['key','label']]`, `canConfirm` = `status === previewed && valid_rows > 0`.
- `confirm` → `$service->confirm($batch)` → redirect show with `"Importación completada: {$batch->created_count} creados, {$batch->updated_count} actualizados"`.
- `cancel` → `$service->cancel($batch)` → redirect index `'Importación cancelada'`.

### Task 25: Importers for suppliers, products and price list items

**Files:**
- Create: `app/Imports/SuppliersImporter.php`, `app/Imports/ProductsImporter.php`, `app/Imports/PriceListItemsImporter.php`, `tests/Feature/DataImport/{SuppliersImporterTest,ProductsImporterTest,PriceListItemsImporterTest}.php`
- Modify: `app/Imports/ImporterRegistry.php` (add the three classes), `app/Http/Controllers/DataImport/ImportController.php` (`metaOptions`: for `price_list_items` return `['price_lists' => PriceList::query()->active()->orderBy('name')->get(['id', 'name'])]`), `resources/js/pages/admin/imports/index.tsx` (render `Select name="price_list_id"` labelled "Lista de precios" above the file input when `importer.meta.price_lists` exists)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: registry types `suppliers` (`catalog.suppliers.import`), `products` (`catalog.products.import`), `price_list_items` (`catalog.price-lists.import`).

Column definitions (`key` / label / required / example):

| Importer | Columns |
|---|---|
| suppliers ("Proveedores") | `code`/Código/no/`PRV-00001`; `name`/Nombre/yes/`Importadora Global LLC`; `country_code`/País/yes/`US`; `tax_id`/RNC/no/`1-01-85004-3`; `foreign_tax_id`/ID fiscal extranjera/no/`US-12-3456789`; `currency_code`/Moneda/yes/`USD`; `credit_days`/Días de crédito/no/`60`; `address`/Dirección/no/`1 Main St`; `phone`/Teléfono/no/`+1 305 555 0100`; `email`/Correo/no/`sales@global.test` |
| products ("Productos") | `sku`/SKU/yes/`RON-750`; `barcode`/Código de barras/no/`7460000000001`; `name`/Nombre/yes/`Ron añejo 750 ml`; `brand_name`/Marca/no/`Brugal`; `category_name`/Categoría/no/`Licores`; `unit_code`/Unidad/yes/`UND`; `units_per_box`/Unidades por caja/no/`12`; `weight_kg`/Peso (kg)/no/`1.2`; `volume_liters`/Volumen (L)/no/`0.75`; `alcohol_degree`/Grado alcohólico/no/`37.5`; `itbis_rate`/ITBIS/yes/`18`; `tracks_lots`/Controla lotes/no/`Sí`; `tariff_code`/Código arancelario/no/`2208.40.00`; `minimum_stock`/Stock mínimo/no/`24`; `additional_tax_codes`/Impuestos adicionales/no/`ISC-ALC;ISC-ADV` |
| price_list_items ("Precios de lista") | `sku`/SKU/yes/`RON-750`; `price`/Precio/yes/`150.50` |

Normalisation and rules:
- **Suppliers**: `country_code` uppercased, rule `Rule::in(array_keys(Countries::OPTIONS))`; `tax_id` normalised; when `country_code === 'DO'` and `tax_id` present → `strlen === 11 ? new DominicanTaxId('cedula') : new DominicanTaxId('rnc')`; when `country_code !== 'DO'` and `tax_id` present → rule `['prohibited']` with message `tax_id.prohibited` → `El RNC/cédula solo aplica a proveedores locales.`; `currency_code` uppercased, `exists:currencies,code`; `credit_days` default `'0'`, `integer|between:0,365`; `email` lowercased, `nullable|email`. Natural key `tax_id ?: foreign_tax_id ?: code ?: null`; `findExisting` by `tax_id`, else `code`. `persist` fills the columns (`currency_id` by code), code auto `PRV-` as in `SaveSupplier` (same `TMP` trick as clients). No override via import.
- **Products**: `sku` uppercased; `brand_name` → `exists:brands,name` (message `brand_name.exists` → `La marca no existe.`), `category_name` → `exists:product_categories,name` (`La categoría no existe.`), `unit_code` uppercased → `exists:units_of_measure,code`; `itbis_rate` normalised (`'18'`, `'18.00'`, `'18%'` → `'18.00'`; `'16'`→`'16.00'`; `'0'`/`'exento'` → `'0.00'`) then `Rule::in(['18.00','16.00','0.00'])`; `tracks_lots` normalised from `sí/si/yes/true/1` → `true`, `no/false/0/''` → `false`; `additional_tax_codes` split on `;`, trimmed, uppercased, each must exist (`withValidator`-style check inside `rules()` via a closure rule: `function ($attr, $value, $fail) { foreach ($codes as $c) if (! AdditionalTax::where('code', $c)->exists()) $fail("Impuesto adicional desconocido: {$c}"); }`), plus the liter/degree consistency messages from Task 17; numeric columns `nullable|numeric|min:0`; `units_per_box` default `'1'`, `integer|min:1`. Natural key `sku`; `findExisting` by sku. `persist` → `app(SaveProduct::class)->handle($attributes, $existing)` with ids resolved from names/codes and `additional_tax_ids` from the codes.
- **Price list items**: `metaRules()` → `['price_list_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:price_lists,id']]`; `sku` uppercased → `exists:products,sku`; `price` → `required|numeric|min:0|regex:/^\d{1,10}(\.\d{1,4})?$/`. Natural key `sku`; `findExisting` → `PriceListItem` for (`meta.price_list_id`, product id). `persist` → `app(SavePriceListItems::class)->handle(PriceList::findOrFail($meta['price_list_id']), [['product_id' => $id, 'price' => $row['price']]])` then return the item.

`SuppliersImporterTest.php`:
```php
<?php

use App\Models\ImportBatch;
use App\Models\Supplier;
use Database\Seeders\CurrencySeeder;

beforeEach(fn () => (new CurrencySeeder)->run());

test('preview and confirm suppliers with create update and invalid rows', function () {
    $existing = Supplier::factory()->create(['country_code' => 'DO', 'tax_id' => '101850043', 'foreign_tax_id' => null, 'name' => 'Viejo']);
    actingAsRole('accounting');
    $file = makeSpreadsheet([
        ['Nombre', 'País', 'RNC', 'ID fiscal extranjera', 'Moneda', 'Días de crédito'],
        ['Nuevo LLC', 'US', '', 'US-1', 'USD', 60],
        ['Actualizado SRL', 'DO', '1-01-85004-3', '', 'DOP', 15],
        ['Mal país', 'ZZ', '', '', 'USD', 0],
        ['Extranjero con RNC', 'US', '131246796', '', 'USD', 0],
    ]);
    $this->post(route('admin.imports.preview', 'suppliers'), ['file' => $file])->assertRedirect();
    $batch = ImportBatch::latest('id')->firstOrFail();
    expect($batch->valid_rows)->toBe(2)->and($batch->invalid_rows)->toBe(2)
        ->and($batch->rows()->where('row_number', 4)->first()->errors)->toHaveKey('country_code')
        ->and($batch->rows()->where('row_number', 5)->first()->errors['tax_id'])->toBe('El RNC/cédula solo aplica a proveedores locales.');

    $this->post(route('admin.imports.confirm', $batch))->assertSessionHas('success', 'Importación completada: 1 creados, 1 actualizados');
    expect(Supplier::where('name', 'Nuevo LLC')->first()->code)->toMatch('/^PRV-\d{5}$/')->and($existing->fresh()->name)->toBe('Actualizado SRL');
});
```

```php
    actingAsRole('accounting');
    $file = makeSpreadsheet([
        ['SKU', 'Nombre', 'Marca', 'Unidad', 'ITBIS', 'Controla lotes', 'Volumen (L)', 'Impuestos adicionales'],
        ['ron-750', 'Ron añejo', 'Brugal', 'und', '18', 'Sí', '0.75', 'ISC-ADV'],
        ['OLD-1', 'Renombrado', '', 'UND', '16', 'No', '', ''],
        ['BAD-1', 'Marca inexistente', 'Nope', 'UND', '18', '', '', ''],
        ['BAD-2', 'Impuesto inexistente', '', 'UND', '18', '', '', 'ZZZ'],
    ]);
    $this->post(route('admin.imports.preview', 'products'), ['file' => $file])->assertRedirect();
    $batch = ImportBatch::latest('id')->firstOrFail();

        ->and($batch->rows()->where('row_number', 4)->first()->errors['brand_name'])->toBe('La marca no existe.')
        ->and($batch->rows()->where('row_number', 5)->first()->errors['additional_tax_codes'])->toBe('Impuesto adicional desconocido: ZZZ');

    $this->post(route('admin.imports.confirm', $batch))->assertSessionHas('success', 'Importación completada: 1 creados, 1 actualizados');

    expect($ron->tracks_lots)->toBeTrue()->and($ron->itbis_rate)->toBe('18.00')->and($ron->additionalTaxes->pluck('code')->all())->toBe(['ISC-ADV'])
        ->and($existing->fresh()->name)->toBe('Renombrado')->and($existing->fresh()->itbis_rate)->toBe('16.00');
});

test('liter tax without volume is invalid', function () {
    UnitOfMeasure::factory()->create(['code' => 'UND']);
    AdditionalTax::factory()->create(['code' => 'ISC-L', 'calculation_type' => 'fixed_per_liter', 'rate' => null, 'amount' => '10']);

    $this->post(route('admin.imports.preview', 'products'), ['file' => $file]);
    expect(ImportBatch::latest('id')->first()->rows()->first()->errors['additional_tax_codes'])->toBe('Los impuestos por litro requieren el volumen en litros del producto.');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Record project rules with Boost (MCP tool `record-rule`, four calls)**
1. glob `app/**`, title `Money is decimal`, note `All amounts, quantities, rates are decimal strings handled by App\Support\Money\Decimal (brick/math, HALF_UP). Never cast money to float. Amounts 2dp, qty/unit prices 4dp, FX 6dp, percents 4dp.`
2. glob `resources/js/**`, title `Spanish UI, English code`, note `All user-facing strings are Spanish (es-DO) with accents; identifiers, routes and files are English. Use @/components/erp/* (DataTable, FormField, PageHeader, ConfirmDelete, ExportMenu, RecordHistory) and Wayfinder routes from @/routes.`
3. glob `tests/**`, title `Tests run on PostgreSQL`, note `Pest 5 on PostgreSQL gomez_test (docker compose service db, port 5435). Use actingAsRole('<role>') and valid<Model>Payload() from tests/Support/helpers.php and crudResourceTests() from tests/Support/crud.php; never sqlite.`
4. glob `app/**`, title `Audit everything`, note `Every model uses App\Models\Concerns\LogsModelActivity; every action/transition/export/import/auth event writes an activity() row with a Spanish description; activity_log is append-only (model guard + PostgreSQL trigger). Every new model or action needs a test asserting its audit rows.`
Then run `php artisan boost:update --no-discover --no-interaction 2>&1 | tail -2` and `test -f .ai/rules/index.md && echo OK` → `OK`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `README.md`**

```markdown
# Gomez ERP

Sistema de gestión para Gomez International (República Dominicana). Laravel 13 · Inertia 3 · React 19 · TypeScript · Tailwind 4 · PostgreSQL 17.

## Desarrollo local
1. `docker compose up -d db` (PostgreSQL en 127.0.0.1:5435; bases `gomez` y `gomez_test`).
2. `composer install && npm install`
3. `cp .env.example .env && php artisan key:generate` — completa `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GOOGLE_WORKSPACE_DOMAIN` y `SUPER_ADMIN_EMAILS`.
4. `php artisan migrate --seed && php artisan storage:link`
5. `composer dev` y abre http://gomezintsystem.test (Herd).

## Pruebas y calidad
`composer test` (Pint + PHPStan + Pest sobre PostgreSQL) · `npm run check` · `npm run types:check`.

## Documentación
Diseño: `docs/superpowers/specs/`. Planes por fase: `docs/superpowers/plans/`.
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit and report** — `chore: phase 1 gate, Boost rules and README`. Report to the coordinator: the commit list (`git log --oneline main..phase-1-base`), the final phpunit summary line, and the four `record-rule` confirmations. The coordinator reviews and merges `phase-1-base` into `main` with `git merge --no-ff`.

## Appendix B — Planner decisions taken in this plan where the spec was open

1. Scaffold via `composer create-project laravel/react-starter-kit --stability=dev --no-scripts` + explicit post-install steps with an empty auth-feature selection (Task 1); Fortify removed (Task 4); login is Socialite-only.
2. PostgreSQL 17 in Docker Compose (Herd Pro is not installed); tests always on PostgreSQL `gomez_test`.
3. Boost-generated files (`.mcp.json`, `boost.json`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/`, `.ai/`) are committed (Task 3; ARCH §10 amended).
4. Role names are English slugs with Spanish labels; Gerencia gets nothing under `admin.*`; warehouse never exports; sales exports only what `visibleTo` allows (Task 5).
5. Super-admin bootstrap from `SUPER_ADMIN_EMAILS` on first login; creating a user is the invitation (mail notice only, no token flow).
6. `hd` is read from Google's userinfo response (server-side); absence of `hd` is rejected (`hd_missing`) even when the email domain matches.
7. Activity log: custom `App\Models\ActivityLog` + PostgreSQL trigger for append-only; request context columns on `activity_log`; `ImportBatchRow` exempt from model auditing (ARCH §6.1 amended).
8. Impersonation in-house (session key), never of another super-admin; `UserInvited` uses the mail channel only (ARCH §7 exception).
9. Money/rounding per ARCH §4; `products.itbis_rate decimal(7,2)` and `alcohol_degree decimal(5,2)` as declared exceptions (ARCH §3 amended).
10. Exchange-rate external connector is a generic HTTP JSON source (URL with `{currency}`/`{date}` + dot JSON path) because no official BCRD JSON API could be verified; daily fetch never overwrites a rate already registered for the day; conversion method is `convertToBase` (ARCH §4/§12 amended).
11. RNC/cédula algorithms from python-stdnum; no whitelist of real numbers failing the checksum — the audited override (permission `catalog.tax-ids.override-validation`, mandatory reason) is the escape hatch; importers never apply it.
12. Suppliers: `tax_id` (RNC/cédula) only for `country_code = 'DO'`; other countries use `foreign_tax_id` (free text).
13. One default price list / one default warehouse; clients with no price list use the default; `financial_accounts` holds both bank accounts and cash boxes.
14. Importers match headers by Spanish label (slugified), max 5000 rows, import only valid rows, upsert by natural key; templates = labels + one example row.
15. Test totals in "Expected" lines are indicative (GC-1); only a failure blocks.
